Compared with conventional mesh-based representations and 3D Gaussian primitives, the proposed meshgrid Gaussian representation better matches the thin-surface property of roads while significantly reducing redundant primitives and overlap during optimization. To further improve representation efficiency and structural fidelity, we introduce a road-structure-aware adaptive meshgrid strategy, which allocates denser Gaussian surfels to geometrically or semantically complex regions, such as lane markings, road boundaries, and height discontinuities, while maintaining a compact representation in flat road areas. Moreover, instead of relying on a single nearest vehicle pose, we design a trajectory-consistency-guided pose-robust refinement strategy, which estimates local surface priors from multiple neighboring poses and adaptively weights pose-guided height regularization according to their geometric consistency.

</details>


### [74] [Beyond Single Expert: Harmonizing Diverse Visual Priors in MLLMs for Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15054)
*Xiao Lin,Xiaohu Huang,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViPS是一个多模态大语言模型框架，通过整合多个基础模型的视觉先验知识（如深度、法线等），并采用高效先验代理和动态先验融合机制，显著提升了空间理解能力，在多项基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常只集成单一基础模型到MLLM中提供先验知识，但作者发现不同基础模型提供的空间先验具有互补性，在不同任务上各有所长。因此，如何有效融合多个异构视觉先验以充分利用其互补优势成为核心动机。

Method: ViPS包含两个关键组件：(1) Efficient Prior Proxy——高效生成多个基础先验（如深度图、法线图等），且推理开销极小；(2) Dynamic Prior Fusion——动态、上下文感知地融合和注入来自不同先验代理的信息，实现和谐的跨先验融合。

Result: 在多个复杂的空间推理和3D空间理解基准测试上，ViPS成功调和了多个异构视觉先验，取得了新的最先进性能（SOTA），实验证明了多先验融合的有效性和动态融合机制的优势。

Conclusion: 不同视觉基础模型提供互补的空间先验，ViPS通过高效生成和动态融合机制充分释放了多先验协同的潜力，是提升MLLM空间理解能力的有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated substantial promise in spatial understanding. Existing works typically incorporate prior knowledge extracted from a pre-trained foundation model to further enhance the spatial awareness of MLLMs. In this paper, we first reveal that when integrating diverse foundation models into MLLMs, different models provide complementary spatial priors that benefit different tasks. Motivated by this, we propose $\textbf{ViPS}$, a novel multi-model prior framework designed to fully unleash the potential of incorporating multiple $\textbf{Vi}$sual $\textbf{P}$riors from diverse models into MLLMs for $\textbf{S}$patial understanding. Specifically, ViPS introduces an Efficient Prior Proxy to generate multiple foundational priors with minimal inference overhead, and a Dynamic Prior Fusion mechanism to achieve harmonious and context-aware prior fusion and injection from the prior proxies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViPS successfully harmonizes diverse visual priors, establishing new state-of-the-art performance across multiple complex spatial reasoning and 3D spatial understanding benchmarks. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/vips

</details>


### [75] [Towards Hierarchical Structure Understanding of Newspaper Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15082)
*William Mocaër,Solène Tarride,Thomas Constum,Merveilles Agbeti-Messan,Tom Simon,Clément Chatelain,Stéphane Nicolas,Pierrick Tranouez,Sébastien Cretin,Thierry Paquet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种报纸结构理解方法：模块化流水线（YOLO + LayoutReader + 自定义分割）和端到端分层Transformer架构Tiramisu，并发布了用于历史报纸层次信息检索评估的Finlam La Liberté数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报纸图像结构复杂（嵌套层次结构、密集异构布局），现有方法难以有效处理，因此需要开发能够理解报纸层次结构的自动化方法。

Method: 1) 模块化自底向上流水线：YOLO检测布局，LayoutReader预测阅读顺序，自定义算法进行文章分割；2) Tiramisu：基于分层Transformer的端到端架构，通过迭代层级过程显式建模文档层次结构，并行注意力机制实现板块/文章分离、块定位、语义分类和阅读顺序预测。

Result: 两种方法均能有效重建复杂报纸层次结构，对比分析展示了各自在大规模文档数字化中的优势。

Conclusion: 模块化流水线和端到端Tiramisu各有优势，前者灵活可解释，后者通过并行注意力高效建模层次；数据集和代码已开源，推动历史报纸结构化理解研究。

Abstract: Understanding newspaper images remains a challenging task due to their complex, nested hierarchical structures and dense, heterogeneous layouts. In this paper, we explore two complementary approaches for newspaper structure understanding. First, we present a modular bottom-up pipeline that combines state-of-the-art open-source models: YOLO for layout detection, LayoutReader for reading order prediction, and a custom algorithm for article segmentation. This approach leverages existing robust components while maintaining flexibility and interpretability. Second, we introduce Tiramisu (Tiered Transformers for Hierarchical Structure Understanding), a novel end-to-end transformer-based architecture that explicitly models document hierarchy through an iterative tiered process. Tiramisu performs section and article separation, block localization, semantic categorization, and reading order prediction using highly parallelized attention mechanisms. Finally, we release Finlam La Liberté, a new dataset designed specifically for evaluating hierarchical information retrieval in historical newspapers. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of both approaches in reconstructing complex newspaper hierarchies, with comparative analysis highlighting their respective strengths for scalable document digitization. The Tiramisu training code, including the synthetic newspaper generator, is available at https://git.litislab.fr/tiramisu/tiramisu-newspaper-articles-extractor.

</details>


### [76] [Quantifying Training Membership Information in the Hyperspherical Embedding Geometry of Face Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15084)
*Ünsal Öztürk,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了人脸识别模型中成员与非成员嵌入向量在超球面上的几何差异，发现训练身份数量是影响成员/非成员可分性的最大因素，且同域参考集上的几何成员信号随身份增加单调递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于角度间隔损失的人脸识别模型在训练时仅对训练身份施加聚类约束，非成员身份可能形成不同几何特性的簇，但缺乏对这种差异大小及其控制因素的量化分析。

Method: 在IResNet骨干网络大小、损失头、训练时长和训练身份数量四个因素的全因子设计下，对180个人脸识别模型计算四个基于簇几何的统计量，并在9个基准上评估每个配置。

Result: 训练身份数量对成员/非成员可分性影响最大，骨干网络和损失头贡献较小；同域参考集上几何成员信号随身份增加单调递减；跨域基准会夸大成员信号；融合四个统计量的学习分类器能揭示更多成员信息。

Conclusion: 训练身份数量是控制成员/非成员几何可分性的关键因素，跨域评估会高估成员信号，多统计量融合可提升成员推断能力。

Abstract: Face recognition models represent each face as an embedding vector on the unit hypersphere by clustering embeddings of the same identity while pushing different identities apart through angular-margin losses. Because these losses act only on training identities, non-member identities may form clusters with different geometric properties. In this paper, we quantify the magnitude of this difference and what training-time factors control it. We compute four statistics based on cluster geometry across 180 face recognition models in a factorial design over IResNet backbone size, loss head, training duration, and the number of training identities, and evaluate each configuration on nine benchmarks. Our results indicate that the number of training identities has the largest effect on member/non-member separability, while backbone and loss head contribute far less, and that, on a same-domain held-out reference, the geometric membership signal decreases monotonically as more identities are added to training. We provide an analysis of cross-domain (pose, age, quality, ethnicity) non-member benchmarks and report that these inflate the apparent membership signal. Finally, we fuse all four statistics with a learned classifier to reveal additional membership information beyond the best individual statistic.

</details>


### [77] [AlphaWiSE: Adaptive Weight Interpolation for Continual Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15094)
*Sarthak Jain,Qiran Hu,Zhen Zhu,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlphaWiSE提出了一种基于权重空间插值的后处理方法，通过为每个参数张量拟合一个共享的插值系数，组合两个冻结的检查点，在不增加推理成本的情况下，有效缓解了多模态模型在持续学习中的跨模态对齐灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型（如CLIP）在持续适应新数据时，会破坏先前阶段学到的跨模态对齐能力。传统持续学习方法只返回单个检查点，强制所有检索方向在稳定性-可塑性之间做出相同的权衡，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出AlphaWiSE，一种后处理的权重空间插值方法。对于两个冻结的源检查点中每个对齐的参数张量（由检查点键标识），拟合一个所有张量条目共享的标量插值系数。系数在小样本记忆集上拟合，用于生成一个插值后的检查点，模型架构和参数量与源检查点相同。

Result: 在音频-图像-文本检索任务上的大量实验表明，AlphaWiSE在多个检索方向和评估指标上持续优于强持续学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了多模态持续学习中的跨模态对齐遗忘问题，通过简单的权重插值策略实现了更优的稳定性-可塑性平衡，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Multimodal models such as CLIP learn a shared embedding space for cross-modal retrieval, but continual adaptation to sequentially arriving data can disrupt the cross-modal alignment acquired from earlier phases. Conventional continual-learning methods return a single checkpoint, which commits every retrieval direction to the same stability-plasticity trade-off. We propose AlphaWiSE, a post-hoc weight-space interpolation method that composes two frozen source checkpoints. For each aligned parameter tensor identified by its checkpoint key, AlphaWiSE fits one scalar interpolation coefficient shared by all tensor entries. The coefficients are fitted on a smaller exemplar memory and used to materialize one interpolated checkpoint. The deployed model has the same architecture and parameter count as either source checkpoint, which does not require additional inference time. Extensive experiments on audio-image-text retrieval show consistent improvements over strong continual-learning baselines across multiple retrieval directions and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [78] [QuReC: All-in-One Image Restoration with Query-Specific Guidance and Local-Global Response Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15097)
*Shen Zhou,Jinghui Zhang,Wenbo Huang,Xuwei Qian,Zhen Wu,Guangwen Peng,Zhiyuan Li,Ding Ding,Dian Shen,Fang Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QuReC是一个统一的图像修复框架，通过退化引导查询重建模块（DQRM）和局部-全局响应校准模块（LGRCM），解决空间异质性和混合退化问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全能图像修复方法依赖图像级提示或共享引导，无法有效处理空间异质性退化（如同一图像中不同区域存在不同类型的退化或混合退化），且缺乏可靠的局部-全局特征聚合机制。

Method: 提出QuReC框架，包含两个核心模块：1) DQRM：将每个空间查询与退化原型空间匹配，重建查询特定的退化感知表示，实现细粒度空间自适应引导；并引入弱监督原型匹配学习策略提升优化稳定性。2) LGRCM：进行局部-全局双分支聚合，并用可学习先验校准聚合响应，增强特征聚合可靠性和局部-全局协调能力。

Result: 在多个全能图像修复基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: QuReC通过细粒度空间自适应引导和可靠的局部-全局特征校准，有效解决了空间异质性和混合退化场景下的全能图像修复问题。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images degraded by multiple corruption types using a single unified model. Existing methods typically rely on image-level prompts or shared guidance to handle diverse degradations. However, such a paradigm becomes inadequate when degradations are spatially heterogeneous or even coexist in mixed forms within a single image. Yet spatially adaptive guidance alone is not sufficient, since accurate restoration also requires each spatial query to reliably aggregate complementary information from local neighborhoods and global contexts. To this end, we propose QuReC, a unified framework for all-in-one image restoration. QuReC consists of a Degradation-Guided Query Reconstruction Module (DQRM) and a Local-Global Response Calibration Module (LGRCM). Specifically, DQRM matches each spatial query against a degradation prototype space to reconstruct a query-specific degradation-aware representation, thereby providing fine-grained spatially adaptive restoration guidance. To further stabilize this query-wise matching process, we introduce a weakly supervised prototype matching learning strategy to improve optimization stability and degradation semantic consistency. Meanwhile, LGRCM performs local-global dual-branch aggregation and calibrates the aggregated responses with learnable priors, improving the reliability of feature aggregation and the coordination between local detail modeling and global context modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that QuReC achieves superior performance on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks. The code is released at https://github.com/zhoushen1/QuReC.

</details>


### [79] [DAPGNet: Dynamic Adaptive Physics-Guided Graph Diffusion Network for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15128)
*Pengkun Wang,Weijia Cao,Ning Wang,Xiaofei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPGNet，一种动态自适应物理引导图扩散网络，通过将光谱物理先验注入图学习（拓扑构建与消息传播），显著提升高光谱图像分类性能，在四个数据集上超越CNN、Transformer、Mamba及图方法基线，平均精度提升3.64-7.31个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的高光谱分类器通常仅从空间邻近性、超像素连通性或特征亲和度构建图拓扑，忽略了连续波段蕴含的光谱物理先验对拓扑估计和消息传播的影响。作者希望将结构约束的物理先验注入关系级图学习，以更好地建模像素关系，应对光谱变异、混合像素和异质边界等挑战。

Method: DAPGNet包含四个关键模块：(1) 多尺度物理先验编码：将连续光谱响应编码为节点级多尺度物理先验表示；(2) 两阶段图构建器：结合光谱-空间亲和度、物理先验一致性和空间距离，形成物理先验感知的稀疏拓扑；(3) 物理门控图扩散：将学习到的边权重转化为加性注意力偏置，通过物理门在节点和特征维度上对图聚合特征与物理先验特征进行插值；(4) 跨尺度融合与多任务优化：融合不同扩散深度的节点状态，联合优化主分类损失、辅助监督损失和二阶光谱平滑正则化。

Result: 在Indian Pines、WHU-Hi-LongKou、Houston2013和Houston2018四个数据集上，DAPGNet在OA、AA和Kappa指标上均优于CNN、Transformer、Mamba及图方法基线。与最强竞争方法相比，AA提升3.64到7.31个百分点。消融和敏感性分析验证了物理先验提取、先验感知拓扑构建、物理门控传播和光谱平滑正则化的互补效果。

Conclusion: 将光谱物理先验以结构约束的方式注入图学习的多个阶段（拓扑构建、消息传播、特征融合），能有效提升高光谱图像分类性能。物理先验与图扩散机制的深度融合是关键的创新点。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) classification requires reliable pixel-relation modeling under spectral variability, mixed pixels, and heterogeneous boundaries. Existing graph-based HSI classifiers usually construct graph topology from spatial proximity, superpixel connectivity, or learned feature affinity. However, the spectral physical prior carried by contiguous bands has limited influence on topology estimation and message propagation. This paper presents DAPGNet, a dynamic adaptive physics-guided graph diffusion network that injects a structure-constrained physical prior into relation-level graph learning. DAPGNet first encodes contiguous spectral responses into node-wise multiscale physical-prior representations. A two-stage graph constructor then combines spectral-spatial affinity, physical-prior consistency, and spatial distance to form a physical-prior-aware sparse topology. During graph diffusion, learned edge weights are transformed into additive attention biases, while a physical gate performs node-wise and feature-wise interpolation between graph-aggregated features and projected physical-prior features. Cross-scale fusion integrates node states from different diffusion depths, and the network is optimized with main classification, auxiliary supervision, and second-order spectral smoothness regularization. Experiments on Indian Pines, WHU-Hi-LongKou, Houston2013, and Houston2018 show that DAPGNet achieves the best OA, AA, and Kappa among representative CNN-, Transformer-, Mamba-, and graph-based baselines. It improves AA over the strongest competing method by 3.64 to 7.31 percentage points across the four datasets. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further support the complementary effects of physical-prior extraction, prior-aware topology construction, physics-gated propagation, and spectral smoothness regularization.

</details>


### [80] [Ray-based phase error correction for miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP under single-directional hyperbolic projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15139)
*Seung-Jae Son,Yatong An,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对微型DOE投影仪的FPP系统中因非线性投影和有限图案控制导致的相位伪影问题，提出了一种基于射线的相位误差校正框架，通过单方向双曲条纹图案估计投影仪针孔几何，并从单一标定位姿构建精化模型，显著提升了非线性投影条件下的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型DOE投影仪在FPP系统中存在非线性投影特性和有限的图案可控性，导致严重的相位伪影，传统图像域处理方法依赖相邻像素，效果不佳。

Method: 提出基于射线的相位误差校正框架，沿投影光线建模相位伪影；引入基于单方向双曲条纹图案的投影仪针孔估计方法，无需立体标定即可恢复投影仪几何；采用数据高效的单一标定位姿构建精化模型。

Result: 在微型DOE投影仪FPP系统上，非线性投影条件下重建精度显著提升，验证了方法的鲁棒性和物理一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于射线、考虑投影仪几何的相位误差校正方法有效解决了微型DOE投影仪的非线性相位伪影问题，且数据效率高。

Abstract: Fringe Projection Profilometry (FPP) systems using miniaturized DOE pro-jectors often suffer from severe phase artifacts due to nonlinear projection characteristics and limited pattern controllability. We propose a ray-based phase error correction framework that models phase artifacts along projection rays from the projector pinhole, incorporating projector geometry without re-lying on image-domain processing or neighboring pixels. A projector pinhole estimation method based on a single-directional hyperbolic fringe pattern is introduced, through which projector geometry can be recovered without stereo calibration. In addition, a data-efficient strategy constructs the re-finement model from a single calibration pose. Experiments on miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP systems demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy under nonlinear projection conditions, confirming the robustness and physical consistency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [81] [MAGiSt3R: Multi-Agent Feed-forward 3D Reconstruction from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15211)
*Ziren Gong,Xiaohan Li,Fabio Tosi,Ninghui Xu,Stefano Mattoccia,Jianfei Cai,Matteo Poggi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGiSt3R是一个多智能体3D重建框架，利用3R系列的前馈模型和MAGMA合并模型，以接近10 FPS的速度从单目RGB视频中进行3D重建和相机跟踪，并通过位姿图优化减少相机漂移，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目视频3D重建方法在速度和精度上存在不足，尤其是相机漂移问题尚未得到有效解决，需要一种高效且准确的重建框架。

Method: 采用3R系列前馈模型处理RGB视频并回归局部点图，提出MAGMA模型在智能体内部和智能体之间合并局部点图，并结合位姿图优化来缓解相机累积漂移。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，MAGiSt3R在3D重建和相机跟踪精度上均超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: MAGiSt3R通过多智能体协同和位姿图优化，实现了高效、准确的单目视频3D重建，为实时应用提供了新方案。

Abstract: This paper presents MAGiSt3R, a multi-agent 3D reconstruction framework performing reconstruction and camera tracking for monocular RGB videos at almost 10 FPS. MAGiSt3R relies on a feed-forward model from the 3R family to process RGB videos and regress local point maps, and on a merging model, MAGMA, that combines local maps at both intra-agent and inter-agent levels to obtain the final global point map. Furthermore, MAGiSt3R performs pose graph optimization to mitigate cumulative camera drift occurring along the feed-forward pipeline. We evaluate MAGiSt3R on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating its superior reconstruction and camera tracking accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [82] [Symbal: Detecting Systematic Misalignments in Model-Generated Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15216)
*Maya Varma,Jean-Benoit Delbrouck,Sophie Ostmeier,Akshay Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Symbal方法，用于检测多模态大模型（MLLM）生成图像描述中的系统性错位错误，并构建了包含170万图像-文本对的SymbalBench基准。Symbal在63.8%的数据集上正确识别系统性错位，性能比基线提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在生成图像描述时常出现错误，导致图像-文本对错位。现有工作缺乏系统性地检测这类与特定视觉特征相关的重复性错误的方法，因此需要自动化工具来审计MLLM生成的描述。

Method: 提出Symbal方法，采用结构化的两阶段流程，利用现成的基座模型来识别系统性错位，并以自然语言总结结果。同时构建SymbalBench基准，包含170万图像-文本对（自然图像和医学图像），覆盖420个视觉语言数据集并标注了系统性错位。

Result: Symbal在63.8%的数据集上正确识别系统性错位，比最接近的基线方法提升近4倍。真实场景评估表明：1) Symbal能准确发现四种MLLM生成描述中的系统性错位；2) Symbal是审计现成图像-描述数据集的有力工具。

Conclusion: 本文提出的新任务、方法和基准有助于用户在不访问底层MLLM的情况下审计其生成的描述并识别关键错误，为多模态大模型的可靠性评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often introduce errors when generating image captions, resulting in misaligned image-text pairs. Our work focuses on a class of captioning errors that we refer to as systematic misalignments, where a recurring error in MLLM-generated captions is closely associated with the presence of a specific visual feature in the paired image. Given a vision-language dataset with MLLM-generated captions, our aim in this work is to detect such errors, a task we refer to as systematic misalignment detection. As our first key contribution, we present Symbal, which utilizes a structured, dual-stage setup with off-the-shelf foundation models to identify systematic misalignments and summarize results in natural language. As our second key contribution, we introduce SymbalBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate automated methods on our proposed task. SymbalBench consists of 1.7 million image-text pairs from two domains (natural and medical images), organized into 420 vision-language datasets with annotated systematic misalignments. Symbal exhibits strong performance on this benchmark, correctly identifying systematic misalignments in 63.8% of datasets, a nearly 4x improvement over the closest baseline. We supplement our evaluations on SymbalBench with real-world evaluations, showing that (1) Symbal can accurately surface systematic misalignments in captions generated by four MLLMs and (2) Symbal is a powerful tool for auditing off-the-shelf image-caption datasets. Ultimately, our novel task, method, and benchmark can aid users with auditing MLLM-generated captions and identifying critical errors, without requiring access to the underlying MLLM. Code is available at https://github.com/Stanford-AIMI/Symbal.

</details>


### [83] [Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning for Unsupervised Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15220)
*Moyao Tian,Shijia Liu,Yan Yang,Xin Yuan,Minshi Chen,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSRL框架，通过结构-语义互惠学习将开环关联转化为自校正闭环系统，解决无监督可见光-红外行人重识别中全局表示模糊和伪标签噪声传播问题，在SYSU-MM01和RegDB上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督跨模态行人重识别方法（渐进式关联范式）依赖模糊的全局表示，且伪标签噪声在开环方式下 unchecked 传播，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出SSRL框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 细粒度结构解耦（FSD），提取判别性身体部位特征作为空间锚点；2) 闭环语义校准（CSC），在每个epoch重建共享语义原型并反馈到训练循环中过滤噪声。两者互惠学习实现鲁棒跨模态表征。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01和RegDB上超越现有SOTA无监督方法，且在RegDB上超过若干有监督方法。

Conclusion: 结构-语义互惠学习能有效解决无监督跨模态ReID中的全局模糊和噪声传播问题，实现闭环自校正。

Abstract: Unsupervised visible-infrared person re-identification (USVI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality gap and the lack of cross-modal identity annotations. Progressive association paradigms have been proposed to gradually bridge the gap, but they suffer from two critical bottlenecks: reliance on ambiguous global representations and unchecked propagation of pseudo-label noise in an open-loop manner. To address these issues, we propose Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning (SSRL), a framework that transforms open-loop association into a self-correcting closed-loop system. Structurally, we introduce Fine-grained Structural Decoupling (FSD) to extract discriminative body-part primitives as reliable spatial anchors, complementing ambiguous holistic silhouettes with spatially consistent structural details. Semantically, we design a Closed-loop Semantic Calibration (CSC) mechanism that reconstructs shared semantic prototypes at each epoch and feeds them back into the training loop, effectively filtering pseudo-label noise before the next clustering cycle. Through the reciprocal interaction between structural and semantic learning, SSRL achieves robust cross-modal representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the competitive performance of SSRL against state-of-the-art USVI-ReID methods on both SYSU-MM01 and RegDB, notably surpassing several supervised counterparts on RegDB.

</details>


### [84] [Divergent Gaze Patterns in Artistic Viewing: Spatial and Temporal Signatures of Attention Across Autistic Individuals, Artists, and Neurotypical Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15227)
*Mohammed Amine Kerkouri,Daphné Senggaran,Renaud Jusiak,Océane Lehmann,Marouane Tliba,Claire Wardak,Emmanuelle Houy-Durand,Shasha Morel-Kohlmeyer,Aladine Chetouani,Nadia Aguillon-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了自闭症成人、训练有素艺术家和神经典型观察者在自由观看30幅画作时的眼动模式，发现艺术家的注视模式与神经典型者几乎无差异，而自闭症群体的注视则在空间上具有广泛探索性但时间上呈现独特模式（更短注视、更少停留、最独特扫描路径），表明自闭症对艺术品的观看是一种独特的群体特异性注意特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有眼动研究多关注自闭症群体在社会场景中的注视行为，而较少探讨其在艺术观看中的表现；同时现有研究多分析注视位置（空间维度），忽略了注视时间和顺序（时间维度）。本研究旨在填补这一空白，系统比较不同群体在艺术自由观看中的时空注意模式。

Method: 研究招募了三组被试（ASD、训练有素艺术家、神经典型者），每人观看30幅画作15秒。采用空间维度（六种显著性指标比较注视密度图）和时间维度（MultiMatch、ScanMatch、FDISS、DTW四种方法比较扫描路径）的双轴分析框架，并用分散阈值算法统一提取注视点。

Result: (i)艺术家与神经典型者在空间（CC=0.96）和时间上几乎无差异，而ASD与两者都不同；(ii)ASD注视存在解离：空间上类似艺术家的广泛探索，但时间上呈现更短注视、更少停留、最独特的扫描路径；(iii)ASD的注视并未在任一指标上表现出选择性类似艺术家的特征。

Conclusion: 自闭症群体对艺术品的观看是一种独特的、群体特异性的注意模式，体现在空间和时间两个维度上，研究结果为构建群体条件化的审美注意模型提供了依据。

Abstract: How different populations visually explore artworks bears on cognitive science and on accessibility design, yet most eye-tracking work in autism has used social scenes rather than art, and has analysed where the eyes land while ignoring when and in what order. We present a comparative free-viewing study across three groups, autistic adults (ASD), trained artists, and neurotypical observers, who each viewed 30 paintings for 15s. We introduce a directed, metric-grounded framework that compares groups along two complementary axes: a spatial axis, in which one group's fixation-density map predicts another's fixations under six saliency metrics (AUC-Judd, NSS, CC, SIM, KL, Information Gain); and a temporal axis, in which individual scanpaths are compared with MultiMatch, ScanMatch, a foveal-disc IoU score (FDISS), and dynamic time warping (DTW). Fixations are extracted uniformly for all groups with a dispersion-threshold algorithm. Three results converge. (i)Artists and neurotypicals are almost indistinguishable in both space (density-map correlation CC=0.96) and time (they form the most alignable scanpath pair), whereas ASD gaze diverges from both. (ii)ASD attention is dissociated: it matches artists' wide spatial exploration (dispersion, explored area) but carries a distinct temporal signature, shorter fixations, less dwell, and the most idiosyncratic (least self-consistent) scanpaths of any group. (iii)ASD gaze is not selectively artist-like on any metric; if anything it is marginally closer to neurotypical. Together these findings indicate that autistic viewing of art is a distinct, group-specific attentional profile in both space and time, and they motivate population-conditioned models of aesthetic attention. We release all analysis code and per-stimulus results.

</details>


### [85] [CRISP: Constrained Refinement via Iterative Squeezing Process for Robust Medical Image Segmentation under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15231)
*Yizhou Fang,Pujin Cheng,Yixiang Liu,Xiaoying Tang,Longxi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRISP框架，利用"正区域排序稳定性"假设，通过隐空间特征扰动生成高精度核心和高召回支撑区域，实现无需目标域数据、无需测试时参数更新的域自适应医学图像分割方法，在心脏MRI和肺部CT血管分割任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中的分布偏移是临床转化的核心瓶颈，现有域自适应方法依赖预定义的模拟偏移或伪监督，难以应对真实世界中无限且不可预测的分布偏移问题。

Method: 基于"正区域排序稳定性"假设，通过隐空间特征扰动提取扰动不变的高精度核心区域和至少一次扰动下保持正性的高召回支撑区域，构建双先验后进行迭代递归优化，实现无需目标域数据、无需测试时参数更新的冻结权重分割。

Result: 在多中心心脏MRI和CT肺部血管分割任务中，CRISP显著优于SOTA方法，HD95在多中心、人口统计、模态偏移上分别降低0.14（7.0%）、1.90（13.1%）、8.39（38.9%）像素。

Conclusion: CRISP作为一种免目标域、免测试时参数更新的即插即用框架，利用排名稳定性假设有效提升了模型在开放世界分布偏移下的鲁棒性，为医学AI的临床部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Distribution shift in medical imaging remains a central bottleneck for the clinical translation of medical AI. Failure to address it can lead to severe performance degradation in unseen environments and exacerbate health inequities. Existing methods for domain adaptation are inherently limited by exhausting predefined possibilities through simulated shifts or pseudo-supervision. Such strategies struggle in the open-ended and unpredictable real world, where distribution shifts are effectively infinite. To address this challenge, we adopt the "Rank Stability of Positive Regions" as a working assumption under distribution shift, and use it to derive robust spatial hints for source-only segmentation. Guided by this assumption, we propose CRISP, a model-agnostic framework that, unlike deployment-time adaptation, requires no test-time parameter updates and no target-domain data--a target-free, plug-in refinement framework that segments with frozen weights. Rather than using ranking to directly output masks, CRISP exploits the stability of probability rankings under distribution shift to derive robust spatial priors. Via latent feature perturbation, perturbation-invariant high-grade regions define a high-precision (HP) core, while voxels that remain potentially foreground under at least one perturbation define a high-recall (HR) support; these dual priors are then recursively refined under perturbation. We then design an iterative training framework that progressively squeezes HP and HR toward the final segmentation. Extensive evaluations on multi-center cardiac MRI and CT-based lung vessel segmentation demonstrate CRISP's superior robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods with striking HD95 reductions of up to 0.14 (7.0% improvement), 1.90 (13.1% improvement), and 8.39 (38.9% improvement) pixels across multi-center, demographic, and modality shifts, respectively.

</details>


### [86] [ARMOR++: Agentic Orchestration of a Multi-Domain Primitive Set for Transferable Attacks on Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15246)
*Christos Korgialas,Gabriel Lee Jun Rong,Dion Jia Xu Ho,Pai Chet Ng,Xiaoxiao Miao,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARMOR++多智能体框架，利用Qwen2.5-VL视觉语言模型提供空间语义先验，Qwen3大语言模型编排五种对抗扰动原语，在黑盒迁移攻击下显著优于现有基线方法，揭示了深度伪造检测器的可靠性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在黑盒迁移攻击下可靠性下降严重，因模型依赖脆弱且架构相关的取证线索。现有攻击方法缺乏语义感知，在无查询约束下难以保持有效性，尤其从卷积代理迁移到Transformer目标时效果不佳。

Method: 提出ARMOR++多智能体框架：1) Qwen2.5-VL视觉语言模型提供空间语义先验；2) Qwen3大语言模型编排原语选择、自适应超参数重参数化和熵正则化扰动混合；3) 集成五种互补攻击原语（密集优化、显著性方法、空间变换、频域扰动、块结构修改）以攻击异构归纳偏置。

Result: 在AADD-2025基准上，ARMOR++在低质量和高质量图像下均显著优于现有智能体与非智能体基线。盲目标攻击成功率(ASR)相比最先进智能体基线有显著提升，在鲁棒防御配置下也表现出进一步性能优势。

Conclusion: 该研究发现当前深度伪造检测器部署中存在显著的可靠性差距，证明了智能体编排在发现潜在漏洞方面的有效性。

Abstract: The reliability of deepfake detectors frequently degrades under black-box adversarial transfer, as these models often rely on fragile, architecture-dependent forensic cues. Existing transfer attacks often lack semantic awareness and struggle to maintain effectiveness under strict no-query constraints, particularly when perturbations are transferred from convolutional surrogates to transformer-based targets. To address these limitations, this paper introduces ARMOR++, a robust multi-agent framework designed for high-transferability deepfake evasion. The framework leverages the Qwen2.5-VL Vision-Language Model (VLM) to supply spatial semantic priors, while the Qwen3 Large Language Model (LLM) orchestrates primitive selection, adaptive hyperparameter reparameterization, and entropy-regularized perturbation mixing. By integrating five complementary primitives, spanning dense optimization, saliency-based methods, spatial transformations, frequency-domain perturbations, and block-structured modifications, ARMOR++ effectively targets heterogeneous inductive biases. Rigorous evaluation on the AADD-2025 benchmark demonstrates that ARMOR++ significantly outperforms existing agentic and non-agentic baselines across both low- and high-quality image regimes. Statistical analysis confirms a substantial gain in blind-target Attack Success Rate (ASR) over the state-of-the-art agentic baseline, with further performance advantages evidenced against non-agentic benchmarks and under robust defensive configurations. These findings highlight a significant residual reliability gap in current deepfake detector deployments and demonstrate the efficacy of agentic orchestration in identifying latent vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [87] [HoloGeo: Mitigating Landmark Bias in Geo-localization via Evidence-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15255)
*Pengcheng Zhou,Xuanyu Liu,Yanchen Yin,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型在地理定位中的地标偏差问题，提出了量化指标BI/BH、基准LandmarkBias-3K，以及基于证据推理的HoloGeo框架和BF-30k数据集，有效提升了定位的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在地理定位中受地标偏差影响，容易忽略地理线索或形成虚假关联，导致定位不准，但缺乏系统性研究和评估指标。

Method: 设计了Bias Intensity (BI)和Bias Harmfulness (BH)两个量化指标，构建LandmarkBias-3K基准；提出HoloGeo证据驱动推理框架，配合BF-30k多证据无偏推理链数据集，通过多维度奖励实现均衡注意力与联合推理。

Result: HoloGeo在IM2GPS3K和YFCC4k上保持优秀性能，在LandmarkBias-3K上显著超越现有开源VLM，验证了鲁棒地理空间推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过量化偏差、构建基准和提出去偏框架，该工作有效缓解了地标偏差问题，增强了VLM地理定位的可靠性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved image geo-localization, yet existing models remain susceptible to landmark bias, causing them to overlook geographical cues or form spurious correlations, ultimately resulting in inaccurate localization. To systematically investigate this issue, we first design two quantitative metrics, Bias Intensity (BI) and Bias Harmfulness (BH), to characterize the impact of landmarks exerted on model reasoning, and establish a comprehensive benchmark, LandmarkBias-3K. To mitigate landmark bias, we further propose an evidence-driven reasoning framework, HoloGeo, to improve the reliability of geo-localization. HoloGeo is supported by a high-quality dataset, BF-30k, annotated with structured multi-evidence bias-free reasoning chains. By incorporating multi-dimensional rewards, HoloGeo explicitly encourages balanced attention over diverse visual cues and achieves evidence-driven joint reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloGeo not only maintains excellent performance on IM2GPS3K and YFCC4k but also significantly outperforms existing open-source VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, validating its effectiveness for robust geospatial reasoning.

</details>


### [88] [SceneBind: Binding What and Where Across Vision, Audio and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15265)
*Mingfei Chen,Zijun Cui,Ruoke Zhang,Hyeonggon Ryu,Eli Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneBind提出了一种全模态场景表示方法，结合语义与3D空间理解，通过语义-空间实体表示、跨模态匹配机制和新型数据集，在场景检索、空间检索和零样本音频-视觉定位等任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态编码器擅长实例级语义理解（"有什么"），但缺乏显式空间结构信息（"在哪里"）。SceneBind旨在弥合这一差距，构建同时包含语义与3D空间理解的场景表示。

Method: 1) 将每个场景表示为语义-空间实体，包含全局语义嵌入和物体级语义-空间槽；2) 提出SceneBind Matching，一种语义-空间匹配方案，整合全局场景相似度与物体对齐；3) 构建真实世界双耳音频-视觉数据集，设计对齐语义与空间信号的训练协议；4) 兼容大规模预训练语义编码器，仅需少量额外token即可添加轻量空间建模。

Result: 在场景检索和空间检索任务上达到SOTA，并在零样本音频-视觉定位等下游任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: SceneBind通过语义-空间联合表示有效弥补了现有全模态模型缺乏空间理解的局限，证明了轻量化的空间建模模块结合预训练语义编码器即可实现强大的跨模态场景理解能力。

Abstract: We present SceneBind, an omni-modal representation of realistic scenes with joint semantic and 3D spatial understanding across vision, audio and language. Existing omni-modal encoders excel at instance-level semantics (i.e., what is present), but often lack explicit spatial structure (i.e., where it is). SceneBind addresses this gap by representing each scene as a semantic-spatial entity, combining a global semantic embedding with object-centric semantic-spatial slots. This representation explicitly captures object-level semantics, spatial attributes, and uncertainty. We further propose SceneBind Matching, a semantic-spatial matching scheme that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment, supporting cross-modal scene retrieval and object grounding. To train and evaluate SceneBind, we curate a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations, and propose a training protocol for aligning semantic and spatial signals across modalities. SceneBind is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders, adds lightweight spatial modeling with only a few additional tokens. It achieves state-of-the-art scene and spatial retrieval while enabling strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.

</details>


### [89] [Motion-Conditioned Multi-View Fusion for Myocardial Infarction Localization from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15268)
*Guang Yang,Wentian Xu,Siyu Wang,Betty Raman,Lei Li,Vicente Grau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MCF-Net，一种运动引导的多视图融合框架，结合超声基础模型与稀疏监督的心肌运动估计，用于心肌梗死节段定位，在F1和准确率上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖手工特征或密集标注，适用性受限；基础模型多基于单视图分析，节段级定位在视图依赖性模糊下不可靠（尤其是心尖切面）。

Method: 1) 使用预训练超声基础模型EchoPrime提取双视图视觉特征；2) 通过单张模板帧跨视频传播进行极稀疏监督的点跟踪，建模心肌运动；3) 运动导出的节段感知软掩膜提供粗粒度空间先验；4) 运动条件化融合机制整合双视图运动与视觉信息。

Result: 节段级MI定位：F1 72.4%，准确率84.9%，优于运动-only、视觉-only及融合基线方法。

Conclusion: MCF-Net通过极稀疏运动监督与多视图融合，有效提升了心肌梗死定位的准确性和鲁棒性，减少了标注负担。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) remains a leading cause of mortality worldwide. Echocardiography (Echo) is a widely available modality for MI assessment, where regional wall motion abnormality is a key indicator. Prior learning based methods for myocardial motion analysis often use handcrafted descriptors or densely supervised estimation, but the need for extensive annotation limits applicability. Foundation models have recently improved vision-based Echo analysis; however, most methods operate on single views and segment-level localization remains unreliable under view-dependent ambiguity, especially in apical views. To address this, we propose MCF-Net, a novel motion-guided multi-view fusion framework that fuses myocardial motion cues with foundation model representations to localize infarction. Visual features are extracted using EchoPrime, a pretrained Echo foundation model shared across dual views. Cardiac motion is modeled with extremely sparse supervision: a single annotated template frame is transferred across videos to initialize point tracking, avoiding dense labels. Motion-derived segment-aware soft masks provide coarse spatial priors that selectively enhance features for challenging myocardial segments. A motion-conditioned fusion mechanism then integrates motion and vision across views, refining predictions without overriding strong appearance cues. On segment-level MI localization, MCF-Net achieves 72.4\% F1 and 84.9\% accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art motion-only, vision-only, and fusion baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Online Neural Space Time Memory for Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15271)
*Baback Elmieh,Lynn Tsai,Zeman Li,Srinivas Kaza,Tiancheng Sun,Gabor Csapo,Ali Behrouz,Yuan Deng,Stephen Lombardi,Steven M. Seitz,Xuan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将記憶更新与記憶应用解耦的在线新视角合成方法，通过周期性記憶更新和逐帧記憶应用，结合跨视角注意力、辅助記憶损失和記憶缓存机制，实现了实时、状态最优的动态场景渲染和长时间記憶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线新视角合成面临长期記憶与实时性的矛盾：标准TTT模型需要每帧进行梯度更新，计算成本高且不稳定。本文旨在降低记忆更新频率，同时保持场景重建质量。

Method: 1) 解耦記憶更新与記憶应用的频率（周期性更新+逐帧应用）；2) 使用跨视角注意力处理記憶状态与当前帧之间的形变；3) 引入辅助記憶损失（Memory Loss）强制模型内化场景；4) 引入記憶缓存（Memory Caching）策略防止权重灾难性漂移。

Result: 在动态人体运动场景和分钟级在线記憶任务上实现了实时、状态最优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦更新与应用频率，并引入稳定机制，该方法在保持高渲染质量的同时突破了实时性瓶颈，适用于长时间动态场景的在线新视角合成。

Abstract: Online novel view synthesis from multi-view streaming videos faces a fundamental trade-off: maintaining a persistent, long-horizon memory to reconstruct temporarily occluded regions while operating under strict real-time constraints. While Test-Time Training (TTT) offers a powerful memory mechanism, standard models mandate gradient-based memory updates at every frame to adapt to the changing motion in dynamic scenes. The computational cost of heavy memory updates precludes real-time application and can lead to instability over long contexts. Given that memory updates are more demanding than memory application and video content is largely redundant, we propose to decouple the frequencies of these two processes. Our approach performs periodic memory updates while applying the memory on a per-frame basis, using cross-view attention to manage deformations between the prior memory state and the current frame. To lock in the historical context, we introduce two critical mechanisms: an auxiliary Memory Loss that forces persistent internalization of the scene, and a Memory Caching strategy that regularizes active weights against catastrophic drift. Our method demonstrates real-time, state-of-the-art performance on scenes with dynamic human motion as well as minute-scale online memorization.

</details>


### [91] [MeanFlowNFT: Bringing Forward-Process RL to Average-Velocity Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15273)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jun Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MeanFlowNFT，将强化学习（RL）对齐方法扩展到MeanFlow生成器上。通过利用MeanFlow恒等式构建瞬时速度预测器，从而应用DiffusionNFT的RL目标函数，同时保持平均速度采样以实现快速少步生成。实验证明，MeanFlowNFT在图像和视频生成任务上显著优于基线，且仅用少量采样步数就能超越多步RL调优的扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeanFlow生成器通过预测时间区间内的平均速度实现了快速少步采样，但现有的RL对齐方法（如DiffusionNFT）主要针对扩散/流模型的瞬时速度进行优化，无法直接应用于以平均速度采样的MeanFlow。作者希望填补这一空白，使MeanFlow也能享受RL带来的偏好对齐收益。

Method: 受MeanFlow恒等式（连接平均速度与瞬时速度）启发，构建一个诱导的瞬时速度预测器；将DiffusionNFT的RL目标函数应用于该瞬时速度预测器，使奖励优化在MeanFlow上变得良定义；采样过程仍使用平均速度，保留MeanFlow快速少步生成的优势；理论上证明了MeanFlowNFT继承了DiffusionNFT的严格策略改进保证。

Result: 在图像和视频生成任务上，MeanFlowNFT一致性地改进了基线；在SD3.5-M的8项指标中，有6项超越了此前最先进的RL调优少步生成器；仅用4步采样即可超越50步的LongCat-Video RL（VBench 84.33 vs 82.57）。

Conclusion: MeanFlowNFT成功将RL对齐引入MeanFlow生成器，既保持了快速少步采样的效率优势，又实现了显著的性能提升，甚至能超越多步RL调优的扩散模型，验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: MeanFlow generators achieve fast few-step sampling by predicting average velocities over time intervals, making them attractive for efficient generation. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful way to align diffusion and flow models with human preferences and task-specific objectives. In particular, DiffusionNFT offers an efficient forward-process RL framework that does not require reverse-process trajectories or likelihood estimation. However, applying such RL methods to MeanFlow remains underexplored. DiffusionNFT optimizes instantaneous velocities, whereas MeanFlow samples with average velocities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MeanFlowNFT. Inspired by the MeanFlow identity, which bridges average and instantaneous velocities, we construct an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor. We apply the DiffusionNFT objective to this predictor, making reward optimization well-defined for MeanFlow. Sampling remains based on the average velocity, preserving MeanFlow's fast few-step generation. We further prove that MeanFlowNFT inherits DiffusionNFT's strict policy-improvement guarantee. Experiments on image and video generation show that MeanFlowNFT consistently improves baselines. Moreover, it outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL-tuned few-step generators on most metrics ($6$ of $8$ on SD3.5-M), and can even surpass multi-step RL-tuned diffusion while using only a few sampling steps. For instance, on Wan 2.1, $4$-step MeanFlowNFT reaches a VBench score of $84.33$, surpassing $50$-step LongCat-Video RL ($82.57$).

</details>


### [92] [Hierarchical Denoising For Multi-Step Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15278)
*Zezhong Qian,Xiaowei Chi,Chak-Wing Mak,Tianze Zhou,Ruibin Yuan,Yuhan Rui,Hengzhe Sun,Zhuoqun Wu,Yuming Li,Siyuan Qian,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HDR是一个将层级潜变量集成到因果视频生成中的统一框架，通过粗到细的推理策略在保持低延迟流式推理的同时，显著提升了多步视觉推理任务的逻辑一致性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型分为流式自回归扩散（推理受限）和双向扩散（推理成本高）两个范式，两者都难以同时实现逻辑一致的推理和低延迟流式输出，缺乏类人的多步推理能力。

Method: 提出层级去噪框架HDR，将视频潜变量组织成树状层级结构：粗去噪层保留不确定性假设进行全局规划，细去噪层逐步精化为具体视觉状态；并设计稀疏层级注意力模式（SHAP）降低时序注意力成本。

Result: 在6个多步推理任务上，HDR比流式自回归扩散基线成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对提升76.2%），平均进度从76.00提升至89.56；推理速度是双向扩散的54.2倍（每潜变量0.70秒）；仅用2%训练数据即可恢复82.9%的全量性能。

Conclusion: HDR通过层级去噪统一了高效流式推理与全局逻辑一致性，在复杂多步视觉推理任务上显著优于现有方法，并展示了在真实机器人物理交互和世界建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Video models are evolving into vision foundation models, yet they still lack human-like multi-step reasoning. Streaming autoregressive diffusion models are efficient but limited in reasoning, while bidirectional diffusion enables global revision with high inference costs due to dense frame-level denoising. Both paradigms struggle to achieve logical consistency and low-latency streaming for complex reasoning tasks. We propose HDR (Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning), a unified framework that integrates hierarchical latents into causal video generation for multi-step reasoning. HDR organizes video latents into a tree-structured hierarchy, enabling coarse-to-fine reasoning before streaming output. Coarse denoising layers preserve uncertain hypotheses for global planning, while finer layers progressively refine them into concrete visual states. A sparse hierarchical attention pattern (SHAP) further reduces temporal attention costs. We introduce a level-stratified multi-step video reasoning benchmark with out-of-distribution cases, covering six tasks: maze navigation, Tower of Hanoi, one-line drawing, sliding puzzle, Sokoban, and water pouring. Compared with streaming autoregressive diffusion baselines, HDR improves success from 34.22 to 60.29 (76.2% relative gain) and increases average progress from 76.00 to 89.56, demonstrating more consistent reasoning trajectories. HDR maintains low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent, achieving 54.2 times faster inference than bidirectional diffusion. It also retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, compared with 52.0% for bidirectional diffusion. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate HDR's potential for physical interaction and world modeling. Project demo: https://hierarchical-diffusion-reasoning.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Adaptive Control of Motor-Position-Controlled Flexible Joint Robots with Uncertain Joint Stiffness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14177)
*Annika Kirner,Grazia Zambella,Igor Kovacevic,Hannes Höppner,Jee-Hwan Ryu,Christian Ott*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对位置控制型柔性关节机器人的自适应控制方法，在线更新关节非线性刚度模型，以应对弹性元件特性随工况变化和老化带来的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中柔性关节的刚度特性会随工况和老化而变化，精确刚度模型难以获得，影响模型控制精度。

Method: 采用隐式控制律和依赖于控制输入的回归矩阵，在线更新每个关节的非线性力矩-转角关系的估计值。

Result: 实验证明该方法在具有非线性刚度特性的柔性关节上有效，并对电机位置控制器引起的误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应控制方法能够有效处理柔性关节刚度不确定性问题，提升模型控制性能。

Abstract: Model-based control of flexible joint robots with position-controlled actuators relies on accurate knowledge of the joint compliance. In practice, precise stiffness models are often unavailable as the properties of physical elastic elements vary with operating conditions and slowly change over time due to wear and aging. To improve model-based control of these systems, we propose an adaptive control approach in this work, which updates an estimate of the uncertain, nonlinear torque-deflection relation of each joint. As opposed to classical adaptive control approaches for non-elastic robots, we rely on an implicit control law and a control-input-dependent regressor matrix to account for the uncertain joint stiffness. We analyze robustness of the approach against errors induced by the motor position controller. Experimental results on a flexible joint with nonlinear stiffness characteristics demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [94] [Semantic Audio-driven Understanding for Dynamic Humanoid Whole Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14182)
*J. M. A. Marcelo,M. Brienza,E. Bugli,L. Comito,D. Nardi,D. D. Bloisi,V. Suriani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态语义音频驱动的人形机器人控制框架，使机器人能够根据音乐或语音输入自主选择和执行运动技能，并在仿真和Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人表演大多基于预编脚本或外部触发行为，缺乏自主性和对动态环境的响应能力，亟需一种能够实时、自主地根据音频输入选择和执行运动技能的控制框架。

Method: 提出多模态编排框架：音频流分为音乐和语音分支；音乐分支通过音频指纹和语义嵌入实现曲目识别与时间对齐；语音分支利用模仿学习技能库实现人机交互；两种模态通过统一接口调度技能执行，底层采用强化学习控制管线。

Result: 在仿真环境和Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了方法，展示了鲁棒的仿真到现实迁移和一致的音频条件策略选择能力。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了人形机器人在动态环境中的自主性和响应能力，实现了语义音频驱动的实时全身运动控制。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics and reinforcement learning have enabled the acquisition of highly expressive whole-body motion policies. However, most robotic performances remain based on pre-scripted sequences or externally triggered behaviors, limiting autonomy and responsiveness to dynamic environments. In this work, we introduce a novel multi-modal orchestration framework for semantic audio-driven humanoid control, enabling robots to autonomously select and execute appropriate motion skills in real time. The system processes continuous audio streams and routes them into music or speech branches. Music input is handled via audio fingerprinting and semantic embeddings to retrieve track identity and temporal alignment, allowing dynamic mapping between musical segments and motion policies. Speech input is grounded into a discrete library of imitation-learned skills, enabling direct human-robot interaction. Both modalities share a unified interface that schedules skill execution over a reinforcement learning control pipeline. We validate the approach in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid, showing robust sim-to-real transfer and consistent audio-conditioned policy selection. Supplementary materials are available at the following site: https://lab-rococo-sapienza.github.io/semantic-WBC/

</details>


### [95] [Open-AoE: An Open Egocentric Manipulation Dataset and Toolchain for Embodied Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14183)
*Zishuo Li,Bowen Yang,Changtao Miao,Kai Zhu,Hao Chen,Qingze Guan,Zhengxing Wu,Wanke Zhan,Yang Sun,Zhiyi Huang,Zitong Shan,Zhenchao Jin,Jiadong Hong,Taowen Wang,Yushi Feng,You Liu,Yibo Wang,Yifan Yang,Zhaowen Zhou,Man Luo,Hao Cheng,Bo Zhang,Jianshu Li,Jiansheng Cai,Guocai Yao,Jize Zhang,Chenhao Lin,Renjing Xu,Lequan Yu,Chao Shen,Chunhua Shen,Zhe Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Open-AoE是一个开放的以自我为中心的操作数据集和工具链，包含2000小时智能手机视频、MANO手部姿态、相机轨迹和原子动作标注，并提供从数据采集到模型训练的完整流程支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源缺乏低成本连续采集、操作级结构化标注和可复用的机器人学习工具链的结合，阻碍了具身智能的发展。

Method: 构建包含2000小时自我中心视频的数据集（500+贡献者、400+智能手机），提供文本标注、MANO手部姿态、相机轨迹和原子动作；开发数据处理流水线（时序动作分割、语义标注、手部重建、相机轨迹重建）和下游工具链（可视化、跨具身重定向、数据转换、训练配方）。

Result: Open-AoE提供了2000小时自然环境下采集的操作视频，并支持VLA策略、WAMs和世界模型等不同架构的训练。

Conclusion: 通过整合可扩展采集、结构化处理和下游适配，Open-AoE降低了数据贡献和复用的门槛，为具身模型训练、人-机器人迁移和世界建模提供了实用的开放基础设施。

Abstract: Egocentric videos of human manipulation provide scalable supervision for embodied intelligence, yet existing resources rarely combine low-cost continuous capture, manipulation-level structured annotations, and reusable tools for robot learning. We present Open-AoE, an open, community-oriented egocentric manipulation dataset and toolchain spanning the full pipeline from smartphone capture to model training. Its first release contains approximately 2,000 hours of manipulation video collected in natural environments by 500+ contributors using 400+ smartphones. The dataset provides text annotations, MANO-based hand poses, camera trajectories, and temporally localized atomic actions. Open-AoE further includes a data processing pipeline that transforms raw recordings into structured samples through temporal action segmentation, semantic annotation, hand reconstruction, and camera trajectory reconstruction. Meanwhile, we provide a separate downstream toolchain supports visualization, cross-embodiment retargeting, model-specific data conversion, and training recipes for VLA policies, WAMs, and World Models. By integrating scalable capture, structured processing, and downstream adaptation, Open-AoE reduces the barriers to both data contribution and reuse, providing practical open infrastructure for embodied model training, human-to-robot transfer, and world modeling.

</details>


### [96] [Never Too Late for Force: Accelerating VLA Post-Training with Reactive Force Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14236)
*Yi Wang,Wendi Chen,Zimo Wen,Han Xue,Xueqi Li,Wenye Yu,Zhijie Chen,Hao Yang,Jun Lv,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIFT通过在后训练阶段注入力觉反馈与在线DAgger微调，使预训练的VLA策略具备接触反应能力，在接触丰富的操作任务中显著优于纯视觉方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略在接触状态下会因遮挡、深度模糊或小力误差导致执行偏离离线演示分布，缺乏接触反应能力。

Method: 在预训练VLA的动作专家旁嫁接一个反应式动作专家，从预训练权重初始化；通过因果力记忆和零初始化交叉注意力注入6D末端力信息；结合在线DAgger循环，在离线对齐数据和人工纠正的在线轨迹混合上训练。

Result: 在毛巾折叠、书籍插入和汉诺塔环放置任务中，LIFT比纯视觉后训练学习更快、性能更高；消融实验表明反应式力记忆和在线纠正数据都至关重要。

Conclusion: LIFT是首个在保持VLA通用操作知识的同时，通过后训练引入力觉接触反应的有效框架。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide strong language-conditioned manipulation knowledge, but they remain largely vision-driven and can struggle once manipulation enters contact states where the scene is occluded, depth is ambiguous, or small force errors push execution off the offline demonstration distribution. We present LIFT (Late Reactive Injection of Force for VLA Post-Training), a force-aware post-training framework that adds contact reactivity to a pretrained VLA policy while preserving its general manipulation knowledge. LIFT grafts a reactive action expert beside the original action expert, initializes it from pretrained action weights, and injects recent 6D end-effector force through causal force memory and zero-initialized cross attention, enabling actions to be refreshed during execution. To address the policy-dependent distribution shift of contact feedback, LIFT further couples reactive force injection with an online DAgger loop that trains on a mixture of offline task-alignment data and human-corrected online rollouts. Across towel folding, book insertion, and Hanoi ring placement, LIFT learns faster and reaches higher performance than vision-only post-training, while ablations show that reactive force memory and online corrective data are both important for robust contact-rich manipulation. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [97] [MEMORA: Embodied Action Memory from Egocentric Videos for Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14252)
*Zihao Yu,Xiu Yuan,Chongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMORA——一种具身动作记忆系统，通过环境、实体、活动和推断知识四类记忆存储，以及在线编辑与离线整合的生命周期管理，为机器人长时程规划提供可编辑、可整合的持久记忆上下文。在EPIC-KITCHENS-100扩展数据集上，MEMORA在记忆评估和规划任务中均显著优于基线模型，并展示了与下游控制结合的实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人规划方法主要依赖当前场景信息进行下一步动作预测，缺乏对人类利用长期记忆（如地点、物体状态变化、操作过程等）进行规划的建模。因此，作者提出具身动作记忆（EAM）概念，旨在赋予机器人形成、维护和利用持久记忆状态的能力，使其能够借鉴过去经验来指导未来决策。

Method: MEMORA包含三个核心阶段：形成（Formation）、整合（Consolidation）和检索（Retrieval）。系统维护四类记忆存储：环境记忆、实体记忆、活动记忆和推断知识。在线编辑在观测到达时持续更新物体身份和状态历史；离线整合则将重复经验抽象为可重用程序和参与者特定的规律。MEMORA-Bench基于EPIC-KITCHENS-100的45小时扩展视频构建，覆盖18名参与者。

Result: 在四种开放权重语言模型上，完整的MEMORA（结合编辑、类型存储和整合）在所有评估记忆条件中取得最强聚合结果。记忆评估准确率比最强控制基线提升最高20.5个百分点；在分布外机器人接地规划（Robot-Grounded Plan）分数上相对提升最高16.6%。此外，双任务机器人部署实验定性展示了记忆接地语言规划与下游控制的结合效果。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，具备可编辑和整合能力的记忆系统能够为机器人规划提供有效的记忆上下文，显著提升规划性能和泛化能力。MEMORA框架为具身智能体赋予长期记忆能力提供了一个可行的范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot planning requires more than predicting what actions will do next; it also requires memory of the embodied experience that makes future goals interpretable. People do not plan from the present scene alone: they draw on remembered places, object-state changes, prior procedures, and regularities revealed through repeated action. We formulate Embodied Action Memory (EAM) as the capability to form, maintain, and use such experience as a persistent memory state for later decisions. MEMORA realizes EAM with a formation-consolidation-retrieval lifecycle and four typed stores: Environment Memory, Entity Memory, Activity Memory, and Inferred Knowledge. Online editing maintains object identities and state histories as new observations arrive; offline consolidation abstracts repeated experience into reusable procedures and participant-specific regularities. MEMORA-Bench evaluates this lifecycle on 45 hours of EPIC-KITCHENS-100 extension video across 18 participants through memory-grounded planning, including previously unseen goals, and a complementary memory-assessment task. Across four open-weight language models, full MEMORA--combining editing, typed stores, and consolidation--achieves the strongest aggregate results among the evaluated memory conditions. It improves memory-assessment accuracy by up to 20.5 points over the strongest controlled baseline and improves out-of-distribution Robot-Grounded Plan score by up to 16.6% relative. A qualitative two-task robot deployment study further illustrates how memory-grounded language plans can interface with downstream control, while the overall results show that editable, consolidated memory can supply remembered context for robot planning. Project page: https://yuzihaowashu.github.io/MEMORA/

</details>


### [98] [Stochastic Filtering for Quorum Sensing in Robot Swarms under Anonymous Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14262)
*Fabio Oddi,Andreagiovanni Reina,Vito Trianni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器人群体中的群体感知（QS）问题，提出了一种受k优先级采样启发的随机过滤协议（ANTk），并与基线匿名协议（AN）和随机变体（ANT）进行了比较。结果表明，AN协议快速但准确性差，ANT提高了准确性但收敛慢，而ANTk通过主动过滤消息缓冲区有效减少临时误差并稳定估计，但错误恢复时间增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人群体需要群体感知（QS）来协调群体层面的活动，但匿名通信协议中由于无法区分消息来源，导致重复消息被重复计数，从而偏倚群体感知估计。因此需要一种改进的协议来提高估计的稳定性和准确性。

Method: 提出了一种受k优先级采样启发的随机过滤协议（ANTk），并与基线匿名协议（AN）和随机变体（ANT）进行对比实验。

Result: 基线协议AN快速但高度不准确；ANT变体提高了准确性但收敛速度慢；ANTk协议成功减少了临时误差并稳定了估计，但错误恢复时间增加。

Conclusion: ANTk协议在群体感知中能有效稳定估计，但需要在误差恢复时间上做出权衡。

Abstract: Quorum Sensing (QS) is a key capability for robot swarms, useful for coordination of activities at the group level. Effective communication is instrumental for individuals to estimate the quorum level of the entire swarm. Anonymous communication protocols where individuals exchange local information without revealing unique identities are helpful to support quorum estimates by sampling information from neighbours and maintain scalability of the QS process. However, because anonymous protocols cannot distinguish message sources, repeated messages from the same sender may be double-counted, thereby biasing collective quorum estimates. In this study, we introduce a stochastic filtering protocol inspired by $k$-priority sampling to improve estimate stability (\ANTk), and we compare it with a baseline anonymous protocols (\AN) and a randomised variant designed to improve accuracy (\ANT). We find that the baseline protocol \AN provides a parsimonious and fast solution, but remains highly inaccurate due to double-counting bias. The \ANT variant improves accuracy but suffers from information inertia, resulting in slower convergence. Finally, actively filtering the message buffer via the \ANTk protocol successfully decreases temporary errors and stabilises the estimate, at the cost of an increased time of recovery from errors.

</details>


### [99] [DiMaS: Distribution Matching for Steering Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14280)
*Pegah Khayatan,Sara Meziane,Jayneel Parekh,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DiMaS，一种针对流匹配视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的分布匹配行为控制策略，解决了传统线性表示操纵方法在机器人操作任务中效果不佳的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA模型在机器人操作中表现强大，但缺乏细粒度行为控制能力（即如何执行任务）。传统线性表示操控方法在语言模型中有效，但在VLA中失效，因此需要新的控制策略。

Method: 提出DiMaS（Distribution-Matching Steering），一种分布匹配策略，通过在表示分布之间进行传输（而非沿固定方向移动）来实现行为控制。该方法利用流匹配的生成过程，在表示空间中进行分布层面的干预。

Result: DiMaS在两种最新VLA模型上有效控制行为；随着训练任务与评估任务差异增大，行为控制的迁移性逐渐减弱；经典的线性操控失效的原因是：行为特征在视觉运动设置中线性可解码但不可线性操控。

Conclusion: 分布匹配策略是控制VLA行为的有效方法，优于传统线性方法；行为控制在任务差异增大时迁移性下降；揭示了VLA表示结构的特性为"线性可解码但不可线性操控"，为未来设计更有效的干预策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Flow-matching-based vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as powerful policies for robotic manipulation, yet a critical capability remains underexplored: fine-grained behavioral control, the ability to govern how a robot performs a task by intervening on its internal representations. Representation steering is a well-established interpretability tool for language and vision-language models, where behavioral features are typically encoded as linear directions, but we show that these classic methods fall short in VLAs. We propose DiMaS, a Distribution-Matching Steering strategy tailored to flow-matching VLAs, which transports between representation distributions rather than shifting along a fixed direction, and show that it effectively controls behavior across two state-of-the-art VLAs. We further examine the generalizability of this strategy as the tasks it is learned from and evaluated on grow increasingly dissimilar, characterizing where behavioral control transfers and where it weakens. Finally, through an analysis of the representation structure of the action expert, we explain why classical linear steering falls short in the visuomotor setting: behavioral features are linearly decodable but not linearly steerable, which motivates the distribution-matching design of DiMaS. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/pegah-kh/dimas, with additional results and videos at https://pegah-kh.github.io/dimas/

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Visual Grasping: Benchmarking Complex Grasping from Detection to Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14341)
*Hanyi Zhang,Khang Nguyen,Charith Munasinghe,Basu Hela,Tianyu Li,Zihong Luo,Hoan Nguyen,Hans Wernher van de Venn,Yalin Zheng,Ravi Prakash,Tung D. Ta,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GCA-Bench，一个面向复杂动作抓取场景的基准，用于评估大模型在多步推理和语义理解下的抓取能力。实验显示现有方法在复杂场景下成功率低于70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取基准仅关注基于视觉的孤立抓取姿态检测，未能覆盖需要多步推理和语义理解的复杂抓取任务，缺乏对大规模模型在复杂动作场景下能力的评估。

Method: 提出GCA-Bench基准，包含场景级推理和语义约束的复杂动作抓取任务；构建多种基线方法（从传统抓取检测到端到端学习）；提出新评估指标并分析关键失败模式。

Result: 在复杂抓取场景下，现有方法成功率均低于70%，表明当前方法存在严重局限性。

Conclusion: GCA-Bench揭示了现有方法在复杂抓取任务中的不足，为开发更鲁棒、可泛化的抓取策略提供了指导和洞察。

Abstract: Robust robotic grasping remains a fundamental challenge for complex real-world applications. Recent advances in large-scale models demonstrate promising capabilities for reasoning in robotic tasks. However, existing benchmarks for grasping primarily focus on isolated, visual-based grasp pose detection, failing to capture the complexity of grasping tasks that require multi-step reasoning and semantic understanding during execution. To address this gap, we propose GCA-Bench, a benchmark featuring challenging \textit{grasping with complex action} scenarios that involve both scene-level reasoning and semantic constraints. GCA-Bench enables the evaluation of recent large foundation models under the same settings. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our new benchmark, we implement a diverse set of baselines, ranging from traditional grasp detection pipelines to end-to-end learning methods. Empirical studies achieve success rates below 70\% on complex grasping scenarios, underscoring critical limitations. In addition, we propose new evaluation metrics, analyze critical failure models, and provide insights to guide the development of more robust and generalizable grasping strategies.

</details>


### [101] [An offline approach to fNIRS-guided reinforcement learning for robot behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14393)
*Julia Santaniello,Madelaine Brower,Benson Jiang,Donatello Sassaroli,Robert Jacob,Jivko Sinapov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了利用功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）脑信号来调控仿真环境中的机器人强化学习。通过被动（观察）和主动（演示）两种交互任务训练智能体，采用参数增强而非替代的方式将神经信号融入RL算法。结果表明，该框架有效：神经信号在增强轨迹优先级和状态-动作Q值时能改善学习效果，并且能从离线数据中成功学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用fNIRS脑信号作为人机交互中的人类反馈信号，探索其调控机器人强化学习的可行性，从而提供一种更直观、自然的对齐用户偏好的方式。

Method: 采用被动（观察）和主动（演示）两种交互任务，将fNIRS脑信号以参数增强（而非替代）的方式融入强化学习算法，重点增强轨迹优先级和状态-动作Q值，并研究模型粒度和噪声对学习的影响。

Result: 神经信号在增强轨迹优先级和状态-动作Q值时显著改善了学习效果；该框架也能从离线数据中成功学习，为实时BCI不可行或数据有限的场景提供了实用替代方案。

Conclusion: 基于fNIRS脑信号的人机协同强化学习框架是可行的，尤其适用于实时BCI设置不切实际或仅有有限数据的场景，为机器人行为对齐用户偏好提供了新途径。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop Reinforcement Learning has become a popular approach to training, finetuning, and aligning robot behavior with user preferences. Our paper explores the feasibility of using brain signals via functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) to modulate robot learning in simulation. We compare agents trained on passive (observational) versus active (demonstrative) interaction tasks, and test multiple methods for enhancing the RL algorithm with the neural signal, focusing on parameter augmentation rather than replacement. We further examine how model granularity and noise affect agent learning. Our results show that this framework is effective: the neural signal improves learning when augmenting trajectory priorities and state-action q-values. Additionally, the framework learns successfully from offline data, offering a practical alternative for settings where real-time BCI setups are impractical or only limited data is available.

</details>


### [102] [ConFlow: Constraints-Guided Learning with Flow Matching for Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14424)
*Nutan Chen,Jianxiang Feng,Marvin Alles,Botond Cseke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ConFlow框架，通过在训练过程中直接整合约束（可微障碍函数/成本函数）和条件高斯过程源分布，解决Flow Matching在机器人运动生成中训练-推理不匹配的问题，在双机器人导航任务中实现更低碰撞率和更优轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Flow Matching在机器人运动生成中仅从数据学习，忽略了许多任务特定的约束（如平滑性、边界条件），导致训练与推理之间存在不匹配。

Method: 1) 将约束信息通过可微障碍函数或成本函数直接融入训练目标；2) 用条件高斯过程替代标准高斯源分布以满足平滑性和边界条件；3) 利用不可行演示作为负样本监督，提升约束满足性。

Result: 在双机器人导航任务中，ConFlow相比标准Flow Matching基线和推理时引导方法，实现了更低的碰撞率和更高的轨迹质量。

Conclusion: 训练时约束整合能有效缩小生成运动模型中训练与推理之间的差距，是一种有前景的范式。

Abstract: In recent years Flow Matching has become a prominent method for generative modeling robot motion generation. In its generic form Flow Matching is an ODE-based neural sampler that is trained by regressing empirical flow fields associated with motion samples as data. However, in robot motion generation we often have additional constraints that might not be present in the collected data. The majority of current approaches train the flow on the available data and use inference-time guidance to enforce task-specific constraints. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{ConFlow}, a constraint-guided flow matching framework that incorporates constraint information directly into the training objective via differentiable barrier or cost functions. To address design specifications such as smoothness and boundary conditions, we propose replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution used in flow matching training with a conditional Gaussian Process. Our approach also uses infeasible demonstrations as negative supervision, improving constraint satisfaction without requiring additional expert data. Experiments on a two-robot navigation task demonstrate that ConFlow achieves lower collision rates and higher trajectory quality than standard flow matching baselines, with or without inference-time guidance. These results validate training-time constraint integration as an effective approach to closing the training--inference gap in generative motion models.

</details>


### [103] [Motion Planning with Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14455)
*Zhilin He,Bowei Li,Jianlin Dou,Yuner Zhang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MD-COAS方法，统一了软约束扩散先验与硬投影算子，并通过自适应调度机制优化安全约束与扩散过程，在非凸环境下的单机器人运动规划中取得更高安全性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的扩散方法在约束环境中存在两个局限：(1) 要么使用软可行性扩散先验，要么使用硬投影算子，缺乏统一框架；(2) 安全强制执行固定不变，忽视了扩散调度的动态变化。因此需要一种统一且自适应的约束优化方法。

Method: 提出MD-COAS，核心包括：(1) 统一非精确增广拉格朗日方法（iALM）的软扩散先验与凸可行集（CFS）的硬投影算子；(2) 自适应调度并协同优化安全强制执行与扩散调度过程。

Result: 在随机生成的高非凸2D基准和7自由度机械臂避障任务上，MD-COAS相比基线规划器实现了更高的安全性与成功率、更快的收敛速度以及更低的最终成本。

Conclusion: 统一软硬约束并引入自适应调度机制，能有效提升扩散模型在复杂约束环境下的运动规划性能。

Abstract: Single-Robot Motion Planning (SRMP) in highly non-convex constrained environments, where robots must satisfy collision-free guarantees, dynamic feasibility, and task-related constraints, is challenging under complex constraints and computational limits. Recent Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) approaches recast the SRMP as trajectory optimization that samples from a posterior over trajectories, using known dynamics, and analytically estimates the score function from rollout samples to guide diffusion denoising toward a low-cost, clean trajectory without demonstration learning. While existing works further adapt MBD to constrained environments and showcase promising performance, they are still limited by (1) enforcing safety either via soft feasibility diffusion priors or hard projection operators, but lack a unified framework to integrate both, and (2) fixing safety enforcement to neglect the changing of diffusion scheduling. Therefore, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion via Constraint Optimization and Adaptive Scheduling (MD-COAS) for SRMP that unifies the inexact Augmented Lagrangian Method (iALM) soft diffusion prior with a Convex Feasible Set (CFS)-based hard projection operator, and adaptively schedules and co-optimizes safety enforcement, along with diffusion scheduling. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves higher safety \& success rates, faster convergence, and lower final costs than baseline planners on randomly generated highly non-convex 2D benchmarks and a 7-DoF robot arm avoidance task.

</details>


### [104] [Mixed-Agent Museum Tour Guide Design Improves Gendered Learning Outcomes and Visitor Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14468)
*Annette M. Masterson,Wonse Jo,Helena C. Sieh,Lionel P. Robert,,Dawn Tilbury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合实体机器人与投影虚拟代理的混合代理导览系统，通过对话与互动实现双移动智能体的交互丰富度。30名参与者的实验表明，该系统在参与度和体验质量上表现一致，但学习效果存在性别差异：混合代理模式显著提升了女性参与者的学习表现。参与者普遍偏好混合代理团队，认为互动性是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博物馆导览机器人多为单一个体，交互丰富度有限。作者希望从单一平台实现双智能体（实体+虚拟）的互动效果，提升参观者的参与度、体验质量和学习效果。

Method: 开发混合代理导览系统（实体机器人+投影虚拟代理）；采用30名参与者的组内实验设计；比较不同对话风格和代理配置；通过问卷、行为传感器和访谈收集数据。

Result: 参与度和体验质量在不同条件下无显著差异；学习效果存在显著的性别调节差异：混合代理条件提升了女性学习表现；所有性别参与者均更偏好混合代理团队。

Conclusion: 提出的双人对话风格对学习效果的影响存在性别差异，但混合代理系统整体上更受用户欢迎，为博物馆等场景的人机交互设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly integrated into everyday contexts, including museums, where they can both entertain and educate visitors. To enhance visitor experience and engagement, we present a novel mixed-agent tour guide system that combines a physical robot with a projected virtual agent that actively participates in the tour through conversation and interaction, achieving the interaction richness of two mobile agents from a single platform. We validate the system through a within-subjects study with 30 participants to assess engagement, quality of experience, and learning performance. Participants experienced different conversational styles and agent configurations, and data were collected via surveys, behavioral sensors, and interviews. Results showed that engagement and quality of experience remained consistent across conditions. Learning performance revealed a significant gender-moderated difference: the mixed-agent conditions improved learning performance for female participants. This suggests that the proposed dyadic conversational style in this paper influenced learning performance differently by gender. Nonetheless, in interviews, participants reported a greater preference for mixed-agent teams regardless of gender, citing interaction as a key factor in their experience.

</details>


### [105] [MIDAS Hand: Modular low-Impedance Direct-drive Anthropomorphic Sensing Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14487)
*Alvin Zhu,Mingzhang Zhu,Beom Jun Kim,Quanyou Wang,Jose Victor S. H. Ramos,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MIDAS Hand，一款低成本、开源、类人尺寸的灵巧手，具有集成触觉传感、16自由度、700克重量、低于3000美元成本，并提供了完整的软硬件栈和评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手硬件难以同时满足类人形态、易制造维护、触觉传感和实用成本，作者旨在提供一个各方面平衡的开源平台。

Method: 采用3D打印组件、直接驱动（低反向驱动扭矩）、16自由度（13主动）、283个三轴触觉传感单元，集成控制API、仿真模型和遥操作管线。

Result: 工作空间与抓取分类分析、负载与可靠性测试、反向驱动测量、带触觉遥操作演示，验证了作为平衡、可复现平台的能力。

Conclusion: MIDAS Hand提供了类人尺度、低成本、可维护、带触觉的灵巧操作平台，有助于推动触觉灵巧操作研究和人-机器人数据采集。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited not only by algorithms but by a shortage of accessible hand hardware that combines human-scale morphology, ease of manufacturing or maintenance, tactile sensing, and practical cost. Existing dexterous hands tend to optimize some of these properties at the expense of others. We present MIDAS Hand, a low-cost, open-source, human-scale dexterous hand with integrated tactile sensing for manipulation research. MIDAS Hand provides 16 total degrees of freedom (DoF) with 13 active DoF, directly driven actuation with measurably low backdrive torque, and 283 three-axis tactile taxels in a compact 700 g package with a bill of materials under 3,000 USD. Built from 3D-printed components, it assembles in under three hours while providing the strength, repeatability, and maintainability needed for repeated real-world experiments. Alongside the hardware, we release a full stack: design files, build documentation, control and tactile Python APIs, simulation models, and retargeting and teleoperation pipelines. We characterize MIDAS Hand through workspace and grasp-taxonomy analysis, payload and reliability tests, backdrivability measurements, and teleoperation demonstrations with tactile sensing, showing that it offers a balanced, reproducible platform for tactile dexterous manipulation and human-to-robot data collection. Project page: https://midas-hand.com

</details>


### [106] [Safe Execution of RL Policies Via Acceleration-Based CBF-QP Constraint Enforcement for Real-World Robotic Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14488)
*Bastien Muraccioli,Alice Cariou,Pierre-Alexandre Leziart,Mathieu Celerier,Arnaud Demont,Gentiane Venture,Mehdi Benallegue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Acc-CBF-QP，一种基于加速度的二次规划安全过滤器，利用控制障碍函数在运行时约束任何强化学习策略以确保安全性，显著减少了机器人（Kinova Gen3 机械臂和 Unitree H1 人形机器人）的约束违反，并已在真实硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人控制中表现优异，但缺乏安全保证，尤其是在腿式机器人和机械臂接近安全边界时，分布外状态会导致部署失败。现有方法难以在运行时统一约束多种安全限制。

Method: 提出 Acc-CBF-QP 安全过滤器，基于二次规划和控制障碍函数，在运行时将任意 RL 策略（包括无约束和 Safe-RL 策略）约束在安全集内。引入 TorqueTask（最小化扭矩偏差）和 Forward Dynamics Task（最小化加速度偏差）两种 QP 任务，在约束违反时调控 RL 命令，统一处理关节位置、速度、扭矩和碰撞约束。

Result: 在真实 H1 人形机器人上，Safe-RL 策略有 10.04 次违反/秒，Acc-CBF-QP 将其降低 92% 至 0.80 次违反/秒。在 Kinova Gen3 机械臂上完全消除违反。在激进速度指令下，延长了机器人存活时间。RL 任务性能在无违反区域得以保持。

Conclusion: Acc-CBF-QP 是一种有效的运行时安全过滤器，能在不修改 RL 训练过程的前提下显著提升安全性，并提供安全-性能权衡的调控手段，适用于多种机器人平台。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated remarkable capabilities for solving complex robotic control problems, but its lack of safety guarantees severely limits deployment on hardware. In particular, as legged robots and manipulators often operate near safety-critical boundaries, out-of-distribution states can lead to failure upon deployment. To address this, we introduce Acc-CBF-QP, an acceleration-based Quadratic Program (QP) safety filter using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) that constrains any RL policy onto a safe set at runtime without modifying training. The method applies to unconstrained and Safe-RL policies, and enforces joint position, velocity, torque, and collision constraints within a unified optimization framework. A key contribution is the formulation of RL+QP tasks that regulate deviation from the RL command when constraints would otherwise be violated. We introduce a TorqueTask, minimizing torque deviation, and a Forward Dynamics Task, minimizing induced acceleration deviation, thus providing principled control over safety-performance trade-offs. Experiments on a 7-DoF Kinova Gen3 manipulator and a 19-DoF Unitree H1 humanoid, both in simulation and on hardware, highlight substantial reductions in constraint violations. On the real H1 hardware, a Safe-RL policy alone yielded 10.04 violations/s, which were reduced by 92% to 0.80 violations/s when augmented with Acc-CBF-QP. On the Kinova Gen3, Acc-CBF-QP fully eliminated violations. Nominal task performance of the RL objective is preserved in violation-free regimes. Under aggressive velocity commands on H1, Acc-CBF-QP improves execution by preventing constraint-induced shutdowns, yielding longer survival times. The full pipeline is open-source.

</details>


### [107] [DRIFT: Drift and Aggregation for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14507)
*Yining Xing,Zhiyuan Liu,Zehong Ke,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT是一种固定深度规划器，通过在紧凑轨迹潜空间中进行单步漂移并结合场景感知的提议聚合，高效生成多假设运动规划，在NAVSIM navtest上达到89.6 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，全模型推理仅需66.43 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端轨迹规划器需要在实时约束下同时表示多种合理的驾驶行为，而将多候选提议集转换为最终规划是一个关键设计难题。

Method: 提出DRIFT规划器：1) DRIFT解码器在潜空间单步生成48个提议特征（32个α=0.5，16个α=0.9）；2) 轻量级聚合头结合场景、导航和自车状态信息直接预测最终轨迹；3) 采用专家轨迹模仿和地图边界正则化训练。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtest上达到89.6 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，具有强可行驶区域合规性和自车进展；提议生成与聚合模块耗时10.82 ms，全模型推理66.43 ms。

Conclusion: 单步潜空间提议生成与直接聚合为多假设运动规划提供了高效的设计方案。

Abstract: End-to-end trajectory planners need to represent multiple plausible driving behaviors while producing a single executable trajectory under real-time constraints. Proposal-based approaches address this ambiguity by generating multiple candidates, but converting the proposal set into a final plan remains a key design problem. We present DRIFT, a fixed-depth planner that combines one-step drifting in a compact trajectory latent space with scene-aware proposal aggregation. Conditioned on features from a pretrained visual encoder, the DRIFT Decoder generates 48 proposal features in a single batched pass, with 32 samples at alpha=0.5 and 16 samples at alpha=0.9. A lightweight Aggregation Head integrates these features with scene, navigation, and ego-state information and directly predicts the final trajectory without requiring trajectory-level quality labels for aggregation. Its output is trained with expert-trajectory imitation and a map-derived boundary regularizer that penalizes waypoints outside the drivable polygon and inside waypoints near its boundary. On NAVSIM navtest, DRIFT achieves 89.6 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, with strong drivable-area compliance and ego progress among the methods compared. The proposal-generation and aggregation module runs in 10.82 ms on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, while full-model inference including the visual backbone takes 66.43 ms. These results show that one-step latent proposal generation and direct aggregation provide an efficient design for multi-hypothesis motion planning.

</details>


### [108] [Communication-Efficient Relative Pose Estimation with Vision Foundation Models for Ephemeral Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14539)
*Qihang Li,Jo-Hao Huang,Jiewen Liu,Suyoung Kang,Hao Zhang,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CERPE系统框架，利用视觉基础模型协同估计自运动和机器人间相对位姿，通过固定大小描述符减少通信开销，并借助度量缩放的自运动传播处理无视觉重叠场景，在短暂协作感知中提升6-DoF相对位姿估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相对位姿估计方法依赖全局参考系、假设持续视觉重叠或通信成本过高，不适用于短暂交互窗口、有限通信带宽及间歇性视觉遮挡的真实多机器人协作场景。

Method: 提出CERPE系统级框架，核心包括：(1) 持续共享固定大小描述符，仅在需要时触发原始图像交换，减少通信量；(2) 利用视觉基础模型联合估计自运动与机器人间相对位姿；(3) 通过度量缩放的自运动传播来维持无视觉重叠时的相对位姿估计。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，CERPE在短暂协作感知（ephemeral collaborative perception）任务中，相较于所选基线方法，在6-DoF相对位姿估计上取得了更优性能。

Conclusion: CERPE有效解决了多机器人短暂相遇场景下的通信高效相对位姿估计问题，验证了视觉基础模型与事件触发通信机制结合的可行性，为实际多机器人协作感知提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Relative pose estimation is a fundamental capability for collaborative perception and coordination in multi-robot systems. However, robots encountering each other in real-world environments often operate in short interaction windows and must operate under limited communication bandwidth with intermittent or missing visual overlap caused by occlusions or limited fields of view. Existing approaches typically rely on global reference frames, assume sustained view overlap, or incur prohibitive communication costs, thereby limiting their applicability to ephemeral collaborative perception. To address these challenges, we introduce communication-efficient relative pose estimation (CERPE), a system-level framework that coordinates vision foundation models to jointly estimate ego-motion and inter-robot relative pose. CERPE reduces unnecessary raw-observation exchange by using continuously shared fixed-size descriptors to gate event-triggered raw-image requests independently of pose estimation. Non-overlapping encounters are handled by propagating inter-robot relative poses through metrically scaled ego-motion, thus maintaining relative pose estimates even in the absence of visual overlap. Experiments in simulation and real-world robots show that CERPE improves 6-DoF relative pose estimation over selected baselines in ephemeral collaborative perception.

</details>


### [109] [SafeRelBench: A Spatial-Relation-Aware Benchmark for Process-Level Safety in VLM-Driven Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14543)
*Huaigang Yang,Ya Li,Min Ren,Bo Dai,Zhenliang Zhang,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFERELBENCH基准，用于评估VLM驱动具身智能体在执行任务过程中的空间关系安全意识，发现现有模型在任务成功与过程安全之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身安全评估主要关注静态风险识别或最终状态任务完成，忽略了由空间关系（如支撑、包含、邻近）引起的过程级安全故障。

Method: 构建SAFERELBENCH基准，包含507个可执行评估样本（248个空间关系样本+259个非空间控制样本），用于评估VLM驱动具身智能体在风险动作前的安全约束满足情况。

Result: 评估7个开源和闭源VLM驱动具身智能体发现，模型在任务成功与过程级安全合规之间存在显著差距，模型常能完成任务但违反过程安全约束。

Conclusion: 安全具身智能不仅需要更强的感知与规划能力，还需要可靠地推理物体关系如何在交互过程中塑造风险。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as the reasoning backbone of embodied agents, enabling robots to interpret visual scenes, follow language instructions, and plan multi-step actions.
