In household environments, however, safety depends not only on recognizing objects, but also on how actions change the physical scene over time. Existing embodied safety evaluations largely focus on static risk recognition, unsafe instruction refusal, or final-state task completion. As a result, process-level safety failures induced by spatial relations such as support, containment, and proximity remain insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce SAFERELBENCH, a spatial-relation-aware safety benchmark with 507 executable evaluation samples, including 248 spatial-relation samples and 259 non-spatial control samples. Using SAFERELBENCH to evaluate seven open- and closed-source VLM-driven embodied agents, we find a substantial gap between task success and process-level safety compliance: models often complete the requested task while violating process-level safety constraints. Unlike prior benchmarks, SAFERELBENCH explicitly tests whether agents satisfy safety conditions before risk-prone actions, making spatial relations a core dimension in embodied safety assessment. More broadly, our results show that safe embodied intelligence requires not only stronger perception and planning, but also reliable reasoning about how object relations shape risk during interaction.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Implicit Force: Evaluating Explicit Force-Torque Proxies in Action Chunking with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14578)
*King Hang Wong,Lingqiao Liu,Feras Dayoub*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了ACT模型在接触丰富的机器人操作任务中是否依赖遥操作追踪误差作为隐式的力觉线索。实验表明，移除该线索会导致力关键阶段严重失败，而添加简单的关节力矩代理（电机电流或关节力）可恢复并提升接触感知行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究ACT在精细操作任务中表现出的"力觉感知"是否实际上依赖于遥操作过程中领导端与跟随端之间的追踪误差这一隐式线索，而非真正的力感知能力。

Method: 提出一种基于观测的ACT变体，预测未来跟随者关节状态而非领导命令，从而消除遥操作引入的偏差信号。同时，使用电机电流或关节力作为简单的力矩代理，评估其能否恢复接触感知行为。

Result: 在四个真实世界任务（表面跟踪、插入、刚度辨别、基于力的停止）中，移除隐式线索导致力关键阶段严重失败；而加入力矩代理后，模型恢复并提升了接触感知能力，甚至超越原ACT策略。

Conclusion: 在真实硬件上，遥操作中的隐式追踪误差是可恢复的力觉信息来源；当力矩信号可用时，简单的代理信号可匹配、超越或进一步增强该隐式线索的效果。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to infer interaction state from signals that are often weakly observable through vision and kinematics alone. Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) has shown strong performance in fine-grained manipulation, but many deployments collect demonstrations through leader-follower teleoperation, where tracking error between commanded leader motion and executed follower motion implicitly encodes contact, resistance, and constraint violation. This paper examines whether ACT's apparent force-awareness depends on this hidden interaction cue. We introduce an observation-centric ACT variant that predicts future follower joint states instead of leader commands, thereby removing the teleoperation-induced discrepancy signal while preserving the rest of the learning pipeline. We then evaluate whether simple joint-torque proxies, derived from onboard motor current or joint effort, can recover contact-aware behavior without external force/torque sensors. Across four real-world tasks spanning surface following, insertion, stiffness discrimination, and force-based stopping, removing the implicit cue leads to severe failures in force-critical phases. In contrast, torque-augmented policies recover robust contact behavior and improve the base ACT policy. These results demonstrate that, on real hardware, the implicit teleoperation cue is a recoverable source of force-awareness, where torque signals are available, a simple proxy matches, surpasses, or further enhances it.

</details>


### [111] [SoftNav: Injecting 3D Scene Tokens into VLMs for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14586)
*Yi Wu,Junjie An,Xiao Liu,Yiqun Zhou,Yuechen Wu,Xiaoqing Guan,Shuyang Yu,You Wang,Guang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftNav通过将实体级3D连续表示（每个检测到的物体或前沿一个token）作为软标记注入VLM的隐藏空间，仅需约1200个样本和约17M可训练参数，在HM3D-OVON上达到74.2%/68.3%/66.7%的成功率，并零样本迁移到多个基准和真实机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过文本将3D场景信息传递给视觉语言模型（VLM），存在表征鸿沟（representation gap），直接嵌入级传输显著优于文本序列化格式。

Method: SoftNav：通过轻量级投影仪，将实体级3D连续表示（每个检测到的物体或前沿一个token）作为软标记注入VLM的隐藏空间。3D编码器和VLM保持冻结状态。

Result: HM3D-OVON上74.2%/68.3%/66.7% SR；零样本迁移到GOAT-Bench（67.2% SR）、SG3D（47.2% s-SR）和真实机器人部署。

Conclusion: 直接将3D场景标记注入VLM能够弥合表征鸿沟，实现仅需少量训练即可迁移的导航能力。

Abstract: In goal-directed embodied navigation, where an agent must locate a specified target in an unseen environment, 3D scene understanding and navigation reasoning must work in concert. Current approaches transmit 3D scene information to vision-language models (VLMs) through text, suggesting a representation gap in our tested configurations; a controlled ablation confirms that direct embedding-level transfer significantly outperforms the evaluated text serialization formats. We introduce SoftNav, which injects entity-level 3D continuous representations -- one token per detected object or frontier -- into a VLM's hidden space as soft tokens through a lightweight projector. With the 3D encoder and VLM frozen, only ~1,200 samples and ~17M trainable parameters are needed. On HM3D-OVON, SoftNav achieves 74.2%/68.3%/66.7% SR across three splits, surpassing all prior methods in both SR and SPL; the same navigation policy transfers zero-shot to GOAT-Bench (67.2% SR), SG3D (47.2% s-SR), and real-world robot deployment without retraining or architectural modification. Injecting 3D scene tokens directly into VLMs bridges the representation gap, enabling transferable navigation with minimal training.

</details>


### [112] [Representation-Aligned Tactile Grounding for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14609)
*Ruilin Chen,Jingkai Jia,Tong Yang,Xinyu Zhou,Qiao Sun,Jiangwei Zhong,Shizeng Zhang,Nuo Chen,Bailin He,Wei Li,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级潜触觉预测器（LTP），通过在交叉注意力动作特征与未来触觉嵌入之间建立表征对齐，解决了触觉增强VLA策略中监督信号应作用于何处的关键问题。实验表明，在中间动作表征上进行触觉接地优于其他对齐方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉增强的VLA策略在接触密集型操作中表现优异，但未来触觉预测的监督信号应作用于何种表征（视觉语言特征、中间动作特征还是最终动作状态）尚不明确。

Method: 通过线性探测分析发现未来触觉状态最可由中间交叉注意力动作特征预测；据此设计LTP，从该中间表征预测紧凑的未来触觉嵌入，避免直接预测噪声原始触觉信号，提供动作-结果接地信号来对齐中间动作表征与未来接触后果。

Result: 真实世界接触密集型操作实验表明，表征对齐的触觉接地优于未对齐或多接口触觉预测方法，验证了触觉监督作用位置的重要性。

Conclusion: 触觉监督应作用于中间动作表征而非最终动作或视觉语言特征，这一发现对设计更高效的触觉增强操作策略具有指导意义。

Abstract: Tactile-enhanced vision-language-action (VLA) policies have been introduced for contact-rich manipulation, where critical interaction states are often hidden from vision. Future tactile prediction is a promising way to use touch because it turns tactile outcomes into supervision for action-induced contact dynamics. Yet VLA policies contain representations with different roles, from perceptual encoding to motor prediction, making it unclear where this supervision should be applied. We study this as a representation-alignment problem. Through a linear probe analysis, we find that future tactile states are most predictable from intermediate action-expert features, rather than from vision-language features or final action states. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a lightweight Latent Tactile Predictor (LTP), which predicts compact future tactile embeddings from the identified intermediate representation. By avoiding direct prediction of noisy raw tactile signals, LTP provides an action-outcome grounding signal that aligns intermediate action representations with future contact consequences. Experiments on real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks show that representation-aligned tactile grounding outperforms less aligned or multi-interface tactile prediction, highlighting the importance of where tactile supervision is applied.

</details>


### [113] [Image-to-Point Cloud Registration Made Easy with Rectified Flow-based LiDAR Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14639)
*Reon Tabata,Kenji Koide,Shuji Oishi,Masashi Yokozuka,Taku Okawara,Aoki Takanose,Jun Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将LiDAR视为成像传感器的新颖I2P配准方法，通过条件整流流生成稠密LiDAR强度图像，再与相机图像进行特征匹配，最终通过PnP-RANSAC估计6自由度位姿。该方法仅需少量LiDAR数据进行微调，无需图像-点云配对或真值位姿，在R3LIVE数据集上达到4.89°/1.63m的平均误差，单次配准耗时约0.68s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像与点云之间存在显著的模态差异，现有I2P方法难以同时兼顾高精度和强泛化能力，亟需一种既能跨越模态鸿沟又具备良好可扩展性的配准方案。

Method: 1) 利用条件整流流从单帧稀疏LiDAR扫描生成稠密LiDAR强度图像；2) 使用预训练的特征匹配器对生成的LiDAR强度图像与相机图像进行匹配；3) 通过PnP-RANSAC估计6自由度相对位姿；4) 模型通过自监督图像补全任务预训练，再在少量LiDAR数据上微调，无需图像-点云配对或传感器真值位姿。

Result: 在R3LIVE数据集上，该方法达到4.89°/1.63m的平均误差，优于现有方法；单次配准仅需约0.68秒。

Conclusion: 将LiDAR视为成像传感器、通过生成强度图像来弥合模态差异是一种简单有效的思路，所提方法在精度和速度上均展现出优越性，且对不同的LiDAR和相机配置具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Image-to-Point Cloud Registration (I2P) is essential for integrating camera and LiDAR in perception and autonomous systems, yet the modality gap between images and point clouds makes it difficult to achieve both high accuracy and strong generalization. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective I2P method that treats LiDAR as an imaging sensor: from a single sparse LiDAR scan, we generate a dense LiDAR intensity image using Conditional Rectified Flow, match it with a camera image using a pre-trained feature matcher, and estimate the 6-DoF relative pose via PnP-RANSAC. The proposed model is pre-trained through a self-supervised image completion task and fine-tuned on a small amount of LiDAR data (neither image-point cloud pairs nor ground-truth sensor poses are required), enabling it to scale to diverse LiDAR and camera configurations. Experiments on the R3LIVE dataset show that the proposed method achieves a mean error of 4.89° / 1.63 m, outperforming existing methods, while completing a single registration in approximately 0.68 s.

</details>


### [114] [NavCMPO: Critic-Guided MeanFlow Policy Optimization for Adaptive Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14643)
*Junjie An,Yi Wu,Xiao Liu,Yiqun Zhou,Yuechen Wu,Xiaoqing Guan,You Wang,Guang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavCMPO是一个两阶段自适应导航框架，结合少步MeanFlow轨迹生成、批评引导优化和强化学习微调，在减少推理延迟的同时显著提升导航成功率，并在真实机器人上验证了有效的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端扩散策略的迭代去噪过程导致推理延迟高，且行为克隆限制了模型性能的上限，无法超越专家演示的质量。

Method: 提出NavCMPO两阶段框架：(1)预训练阶段：通过障碍物接近度预测任务学习障碍感知的视觉表示，采用少步MeanFlow轨迹生成配合批评引导轨迹优化；(2)适应阶段：使用PPO算法结合行为克隆正则化微调策略，并更新批评器以适应具体机器人观测变化。

Result: 在InternVLA-N1基准上，NavCMPO平均成功率达74.7%，超过NavDP基线6.4个百分点，推理延迟从85ms降至60ms；在Unitree Go2机器人上验证了有效的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: NavCMPO通过结合扩散模型少步生成、批评引导优化和强化学习微调，有效解决了端到端扩散策略的推理延迟高和性能受限问题，在降低延迟的同时提升了导航性能，并具备良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: End-to-end diffusion-based policies have demonstrated strong performance in mapless visual navigation, but their iterative denoising process introduces substantial inference latency, while behavior cloning limits performance to the quality of expert demonstrations. We present NavCMPO, a two-stage adaptive navigation framework that combines few-step MeanFlow trajectory generation, critic-guided refinement, and reinforcement learning fine-tuning. During pre-training, an obstacle proximity prediction task encourages the visual representation to capture obstacle-aware spatial information. To compensate for the degradation in obstacle avoidance caused by few-step generation, Critic-Guided Trajectory Refinement (CGTR) uses gradients from a critic trained with obstacle-point-cloud supervision to refine intermediate trajectories. During adaptation, the MeanFlow policy is fine-tuned using Proximal Policy Optimization with behavior-cloning regularization, while the critic is updated to accommodate embodiment-specific observation changes. Under a matched training budget on the InternVLA-N1 benchmark, NavCMPO achieves an average success rate of 74.7\%, exceeding the retrained NavDP baseline by 6.4 percentage points, while reducing inference latency from 85\,ms to 60\,ms. Experiments on a Unitree Go2 further demonstrate effective sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [115] [An Intelligent-Cloud Edge Multimodal Interaction System for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14675)
*Zihan Guo,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种云边多模态交互框架，集成改进的YOLO-DC手势检测器与LLM/VLM智能体，在资源受限的机器人上实现鲁棒的人机交互，在公共数据集和自定义数据集上分别达到98.9%和95.0%的精确度，系统级任务成功率达82-95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂环境下机器人手势感知、场景理解与任务规划在有限计算资源下的挑战，实现鲁棒的人机交互。

Method: 提出云边多模态交互框架：云层负责手势检测、场景理解、多模态融合和动作规划；TonyPi机器人本地处理数据采集、通信和动作执行。改进YOLO-DC检测器，在颈部引入CBAM注意力模块并用DIoU损失替换回归目标。

Result: YOLO-DC在公共数据集和自定义数据集上精确度达98.9%和95.0%，mAP@0.5为90.7%和92.7%。系统级成功率：单动作95%、复合动作88%、视觉依赖任务82%。用户满意度平均3.69/5分。

Conclusion: 证明了将精细化手势检测与多模态智能体结合，在资源受限的机器人交互场景中具有可行性。

Abstract: Robust human-robot interaction in complex environments requires accurate gesture perception, semantic scene understanding, and reliable task planning under limited onboard computing resources. This paper presents a cloud-edge multimodal interaction framework that integrates an enhanced YOLO-based gesture detector with coordinated large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) agents. The proposed detector, incorporates the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) into the neck and replaces the baseline bounding-box regression objective with Distance-IoU (DIoU) loss. These modifications improve feature discrimination and localization for small or partially occluded gestures in complex backgrounds. The cloud layer performs gesture detection, scene understanding, multimodal fusion, and action planning, whereas the TonyPi robot locally handles data acquisition, communication, action execution, and feedback. Experiments on a public gesture dataset and a custom dataset show that YOLO-DC achieves precision values of 98.9% and 95.0%, with mAP@0.5 values of 90.7% and 92.7%, respectively. System-level evaluation yields success rates of 95%, 88%, and 82% for single-action, composite-action, and vision-dependent tasks. A 30 participant evaluation yields an overall mean satisfaction score of 3.69 out of 5. These results demonstrate the feasibility of combining refined gesture detection with multimodal agents for resource-constrained robotic interaction.

</details>


### [116] [MIND-CAVs: Multi-Intelligence Negotiation and Decision System for CAVs based on Intent-Driven Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14688)
*Mainak Mondal,Yihang Feng,Yangchao Luo,Han Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND-CAVs框架，通过意图驱动自治、V2X通信和边缘计算实现多辆联网自动驾驶车辆之间的全局一致性协调决策，在CARLA仿真中相比基线方法提升了机动完成时间并降低了不安全接近和不必要制动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶车辆大多作为孤立智能体运行，仅广播低级运动状态，缺乏传达高级机动意图的机制；现有协同驾驶框架仅实现有限的传感器共享，且边缘计算主要用于内容分发而非决策仲裁，导致无法实现全局一致的、意图感知的协调决策。

Method: 提出MIND-CAVs框架，包含三层架构：车辆层将原始传感器观测抽象为结构化意图表示并通过V2X链路交换；边缘层结合学习与规则仲裁机制协调车辆间的冲突意图；云端记录决策用于审计和持续重训练。在CARLA中搭建AI-in-the-loop仿真平台进行评估。

Result: 在多车道高速公路场景中，相比孤立自治、先到先服务仲裁和多智能体强化学习基线，MIND-CAVs在机动完成时间、不安全接近和必要制动的减少方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: MIND-CAVs通过意图驱动自治和边缘仲裁有效解决了联网自动驾驶车辆间的协调问题，为未来智能交通系统中的全局一致性决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern autonomous vehicles largely operate as isolated agents: they rely on on-board perception and decision modules and broadcast Basic Safety Messages (BSMs) that expose only low-level kinematic state. While existing cooperative driving frameworks enable limited sensor sharing, they rarely communicate high-level maneuver intentions, and edge computing is primarily used for content delivery rather than decision arbitration. As a result, current connected autonomy lacks a principled mechanism for making globally consistent, intent-aware coordination decisions across vehicles. To address this gap, we propose MIND-CAVs, a Multi-Intelligence Negotiation and Decision framework for connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs) based on intent-driven autonomy. Each vehicle abstracts raw sensor observations into structured intent representations, exchanges them over V2X links, and receives globally consistent coordination plans from roadside edge servers. Edge agents combine learned and rule-based arbitration mechanisms to negotiate conflicting intents among vehicles, while a cloud platform records decisions for auditing and continual retraining. We implement MIND-CAVs in a CARLA-based AI-in-the-loop platform and evaluate it in multi-lane highway scenarios involving conflicting maneuvers and route-constrained exits. Experimental results show improved maneuver completion time and reduced unsafe proximity and unnecessary braking compared with isolated autonomy, first-come-first-served arbitration, and multi-agent reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Reflex: Real-Time VLA Control through Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14695)
*Yuanchun Guo,Bingyan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Reflex提出了一种针对流匹配VLA模型的实时流式推理框架，通过利用时间步不变性、分区注意力、自适应归一化、异步流水线和算子融合等技术，实现了2.58倍加速和50Hz稳定流式推理，同时保持性能不下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA模型虽然能实现精确的连续控制，但其迭代去噪的本质与实时机器人系统存在根本性不兼容：全局时间步注入使得KV缓存失效，导致要么选择慢速的O(N²)重计算，要么使用数学上不正确的缓存复用。

Method: 1) 利用时间步不变性，将感知编码器与去噪循环解耦；2) 将注意力上下文划分为静态、滑动和动态区域，实现O(1)增量缓存更新；3) 提出AdaRMSNorm自适应归一化层防止BFloat16数值坍塌；4) 设计异步流水线解耦视觉编码与动作生成；5) 采用算子融合减少内核开销。

Result: 在LIBERO和Kinetix基准上，Reflex实现了2.58倍推理加速和50Hz稳定流式推理，反应延迟降低高达54%，且无性能退化。

Conclusion: Reflex通过创新的推理优化技术，有效解决了流匹配VLA模型在实时机器人部署中的核心瓶颈，实现了高效、稳定的实时流式推理，为机器人策略部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Flow matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise precise continuous control, but their iterative denoising nature introduces fundamental incompatibilities with real-time robotics: global timestep injection invalidates KV-caching, forcing a choice between slow $O(N^2)$ re-computation or mathematically incorrect cache reuse. We present \textbf{Reflex}, a framework that enables \textit{real-time streaming inference} for flow matching policies by exploiting the \textit{Timestep-Invariance Property} -- that perception encoders are functionally independent of the denoising loop. Reflex partitions the attention context into static, sliding, and dynamic regions, enabling $O(1)$ incremental cache updates while preserving full-batch-equivalent attention outputs for fixed inputs. To ensure stability under continuous high-frequency inference, we introduce \textit{AdaRMSNorm}, an adaptive normalization layer that prevents BFloat16 numerical collapse by gating on flow phase. We further maximize throughput through an \textit{async pipeline} that decouples visual encoding from action generation, combined with \textit{operator fusion} that reduces kernel overhead. On LIBERO and Kinetix benchmarks, Reflex achieves a 2.58$\times$ inference speedup and 50Hz stable streaming, reducing reaction latency by up to 54\% and enabling efficient deployment without performance degradation.

</details>


### [118] [Lights, Camera, Malfunction: When Illumination Robustness Leaves VLA Models Blind to Color](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14698)
*Marino Watanabe,Takami Sato,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出FLARE物理光照攻击框架可使VLA模型任务成功率降至0%，并发现传统对抗训练中的数据增强会导致模型颜色感知崩溃（形状偏置）。为此提出ChromaGuard颜色保持对抗训练方法，在物理机器人上实现97.5%（正常）和92.5%（受攻击）的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实环境中对微小扰动（如光照变化）存在脆弱性，现有对抗训练方法（数据增强）会意外地让模型丢弃颜色信息，退化为纯形状偏置处理器，严重损害颜色依赖的真实任务性能。

Method: 1) FLARE：无需模型内部访问的优化物理聚光灯攻击框架；2) ChromaGuard：颜色保持对抗训练方法，保留颜色信息的同时防御光照攻击。

Result: FLARE攻击使基准任务成功率降至0%；传统对抗训练后模型在灰度输入上保持高成功率，但在颜色依赖的真实任务上成功率降至≤47.5%；ChromaGuard在正常和受攻击情况下分别达到97.5%和92.5%的成功率。

Conclusion: 揭示了VLA模型对抗训练中颜色信息丢失的关键缺陷，证明了颜色保持的对抗训练对鲁棒泛化至关重要。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for general-purpose robot manipulation; however, their transition to real-world environments reveals vulnerabilities to minor environmental perturbations. We propose FLARE, an optimized physical spotlight attack framework that exploits these vulnerabilities via targeted illuminations, dropping baseline task success rates to zero without any access to model internals. While adversarial training is the standard countermeasure, we identify a critical and previously underestimated defensive pitfall: naive data augmentations incorrectly condition VLA models to discard color as noise, collapsing their visual perception into a purely shape-biased processor. We expose this degradation through a diagnostic grayscale evaluation, in which the defended model maintains high success rates on grayscale inputs, while its success rate on benign, color-dependent real-world tasks drops to at most 47.5%, well below the undefended baseline. To address this, we propose ChromaGuard, a chroma-preserving adversarial training method. On a physical 6-DoF robotic platform, we demonstrate that ChromaGuard achieves 97.5% and 92.5% success rates in benign and attacked color-dependent tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [119] [Reinforcement Learning for the Full Strawberry Harvesting Process: Obstacle Separation, Detachment, and Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14708)
*Changyou Miao,Teng Li,Ya Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于策略驱动的强化学习框架，结合启发式相位协调，用于解决草莓采摘中严重遮挡和可变形结构带来的复杂接触动力学问题。在仿真中成功率达89.7%，在真实实验中达82.0%，并实现了零样本从仿真到真实平台的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草莓采摘面临严重遮挡和可变形植物结构带来的复杂接触动力学挑战，现有方法难以有效处理此类问题。

Method: 提出策略驱动强化学习框架，将障碍分离、果实摘除和放置建模为顺序决策任务；使用共享交互感知策略生成笛卡尔运动，结合轻量级启发式逻辑协调任务进度；采用层级架构，高层策略与低层笛卡尔阻抗控制结合；采用可行性优先的观测对齐和域随机化实现零样本仿真到真实迁移。

Result: 仿真成功率达89.7%，真实实验成功率达82.0%。随着遮挡程度从1增加到5，平均执行时间从12.99秒增加到21.73秒。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了将交互感知的采摘行为迁移到结构不同的机器人平台上，证明了其在复杂环境下草莓采摘的有效性。

Abstract: Severe occlusions and deformable plant structures introduce complex contact dynamics that challenge robotic strawberry harvesting. A policy-driven reinforcement learning (RL) framework with heuristic phase coordination was developed, in which obstacle separation, fruit detachment, and placement were formulated as a sequential decision-making task. A shared interaction-aware policy generated Cartesian motions across all task phases, while lightweight heuristic logic coordinated task progression and gripper events. A shared structured observation space was used to represent target, obstacle, end-effector, and task-context information. A hierarchical architecture combined the high-level policy with low-level Cartesian impedance control for compliant interaction. To support zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, feasibility-first observation alignment and domain randomization were adopted. The policy achieved success rates of 89.7% in simulation and 82.0% in real-world experiments. As the occlusion level increased from 1 to 5, the average execution time increased from 12.99 s to 21.73 s, reflecting greater interaction complexity. These results demonstrated effective transfer of interaction-aware harvesting behaviors to a structurally different robotic platform.

</details>


### [120] [BridgeFlow: Fast and Robust SE(2)-Equivariant Motion Planning with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14725)
*Xinzhe Zhou,Xuyang Wang,Xiaoming Duan,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BridgeFlow是一种快速且严格SE(2)-等变的生成式运动规划框架，通过轻量级任务中心规范化模块实现精确空间等变性，结合布朗桥先验和上下文感知小批量最优传输加速推理，在2D环境和7自由度Franka机械臂上实现了高达15倍推理加速和2倍有效轨迹率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的运动规划器面临两难：要么缺乏内在等变性（将变换后的任务视为新场景），要么通过计算昂贵的专用架构实现等变，这限制了实时推理。作者旨在打破这种权衡。

Method: 提出BridgeFlow框架：(1) 轻量级任务中心规范化模块实现精确SE(2)空间等变性；(2) 布朗桥信息先验 + 上下文感知小批量最优传输，构建直线化向量场以最小化传输成本并稳定训练；(3) 通过分类器无导引显式嵌入环境感知。

Result: 在密集2D环境和7-DoF Franka机械臂上，相比最先进的扩散基线，BridgeFlow实现了高达15倍推理加速和2倍更高的有效轨迹率，并能鲁棒泛化到完全未见环境和任意空间变换。

Conclusion: BridgeFlow成功打破了等变性与计算效率之间的权衡，证明通过轻量级规范化模块而非重等变网络即可实现严格等变，同时大幅加速推理并提升轨迹质量。

Abstract: In robotic motion planning, equivariance to rigid body transformations is crucial for robust spatial generalization. However, current learning-based planners face a critical dilemma: they either lack inherent equivariance, treating transformed tasks as novel scenarios, or enforce it via computationally expensive specialized architectures that bottleneck real-time inference. To break this trade-off, we propose BridgeFlow, a fast and strictly SE(2)-equivariant generative motion planning framework. Rather than relying on heavy equivariant networks, BridgeFlow achieves exact spatial equivariance via a lightweight task-centric canonicalization module, enabling generalization using standard architectures. To further accelerate inference, we pair a Brownian bridge informative prior with context-aware mini-batch optimal transport. This constructs a straightened vector field that minimizes transport costs and stabilizes training. Furthermore, environmental awareness is explicitly embedded via Classifier-Free Guidance. Evaluations in dense 2D environments and on a 7-DoF Franka manipulator demonstrate that BridgeFlow achieves up to a 15x inference speedup and a 2x higher valid trajectory rate over state-of-the-art diffusion baselines, alongside robust generalization to entirely unseen environments and arbitrary spatial transformations.

</details>


### [121] [Hybrid Rigid-Soft Robotic Gripper with Shape Adaptation, Uniform Force Distribution, and Self-Locking Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14730)
*Xi Chen,Yun Wang,Lichao Yang,Haitao Li,Ya Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合刚柔机械手，集成气动软体执行器与3D打印双棘轮-棘爪自锁机构，在4200g负载（远超传统软体手45-210g）、均匀力分布（差异比1.75-35.29%）、能耗降低50.05%（42.6J/次）方面取得突破，实现形状自适应与无能耗自锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决农业自动化中传统机械手在柔顺抓取、压力均衡与高负载能力之间的权衡难题，特别是克服软体机械手负载低、能耗高的局限性。

Method: 将低成本膜式气动执行器与3D打印双棘轮-棘爪机构集成，采用偏置装配提高关节锁定角分辨率，通过自锁机制实现抓取后无需持续气压维持。

Result: 最大负载4200g（远超市面软体手45-210g）；力分布差异比1.75-35.29%（刚性手为56.77-66.44%）；单次抓取能耗42.6J，较传统软体手85.28J降低50.05%。

Conclusion: 该混合设计有效弥合了软体顺应性与刚性可靠性之间的差距，为可扩展的农业自动化收割与操作任务提供了低成本、高效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional robotic grippers face a significant challenge in agricultural automation: the trade-off between compliant, adaptive grasping, pressure balancing among all joints, and high load capacity, often at the cost of high energy consumption. This paper presents a novel hybrid rigid-soft gripper that integrated low-cost, membrane-based pneumatic actuators with 3D-printed dual ratchet-pawl mechanisms to simultaneously achieve shape adaptation, uniform force distribution, and energy-free self-locking. The dual-ratchet structure assembled in an offset configuration significantly increased the angular resolution of the joint locking mechanism. Key experimental results demonstrated the gripper's superior performance: a remarkable maximum load capacity of 4200 g, far exceeding that of conventional soft grippers (45-210 g); more uniform force distribution across object sizes (1.75-35.29% difference ratio) compared to a rigid gripper (56.77-66.44%), with peak contact forces remaining below surface damage thresholds; and a 50.05% reduction in total energy consumption to 42.6 J per grasp cycle, achieved by eliminating the need for continuous pneumatic pressure through the self-locking mechanism, compared to 85.28 J for a conventional soft gripper. The combination of additive manufacturing for ratchets and commercially available materials for pneumatic chambers ensured a low-cost and easily fabricated design. These findings validated that the proposed gripper successfully bridged the gap between soft compliance and rigid reliability, offering a robust and efficient solution for scalable agricultural harvesting and manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Curvature-Constrained and Constant-Speed Distributed Simultaneous Arrival Control for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14781)
*Zhouru Xiao,Yang Lu,Weijia Yao,Min Liu,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对曲率受限且速度恒定的多机器人系统（如固定翼无人机），提出了一种基于最大一致性协议的分布式切换控制方法，通过引入虚拟时间变量并结合Dubins路径几何特性与优化原理，实现了机器人在温和条件下的同时到达目标点，并在特定情况下达到理论最优到达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人同时到达目标点对于合作围捕、灾难救援和环境监测等协作任务至关重要，而现有方法难以同时处理轨迹曲率约束、恒定速度（各机器人速度可能不同）以及分布式控制等实际约束。

Method: 提出一种基于最大一致性协议的分布式切换控制方法。利用Dubins路径几何特性与优化原理，引入虚拟时间变量，设计了融合最优控制与饱和比例控制的混合控制律，驱动每个机器人接近邻居中的最大虚拟时间。

Result: 在温和条件下实现了多机器人同时到达；在特定情况下达到理论最优到达时间；方法具有可扩展性、实时性和低通信开销；通过大量仿真和实验验证了有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式切换控制方法有效解决了曲率受限、速度恒定的多机器人系统的同时到达问题，具有理论最优性保证，且适用于实际固定翼无人机等平台。

Abstract: The simultaneous arrival of multiple mobile robots at a target point is crucial for cooperation tasks such as cooperative encirclement, disaster relief, and environmental monitoring. Although the simultaneous arrival problem itself is already complex, the problem becomes more challenging when there are constraints on the robot trajectory curvatures and the speeds are required to be constant (possibly different for different robots), and the control law for robots needs to be distributed. These constraints are typical for a multi-robot system consisting of, e.g., fixed-wing UAVs. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a distributed switching control method based on the maximum consensus protocol. By exploiting the geometric properties of Dubins paths along with optimization principles, a virtual time variable is introduced, and a hybrid control law that combines optimal control with saturated proportional control is designed. Under the proposed control law, each robot is driven to approach the maximum virtual time among its neighbors, thereby achieving simultaneous arrival under some mild conditions. Furthermore, we prove that in certain cases the proposed method attains a theoretically optimal arrival time. The approach is scalable and real-time, with low communication overhead. Its effectiveness and robustness are validated through extensive simulations and experiments.

</details>


### [123] [Interventional Causal Circuits for Safe Robot Action Testing and Failure Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14826)
*Naren Vasantakumaar,Tom Schierenbeck,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个闭环框架，将联合概率树（JPT）与因果电路耦合，在机器人动作被测试拒绝时进行因果诊断，精确定位失败参数并提供修正值，无需重训练或额外数据。实验显示可减少10.3%-37%的失败尝试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人运动参数的形式化测试计算成本高，且高维动作空间下可扩展性差。当动作被拒绝时，简单的随机重采样效率低、无信息且不收敛。亟需一种能因果诊断失败原因并给出修正建议的框架。

Method: 提出闭环框架，将联合概率树（JPT）与从边际-确定性变量树推导出的因果电路耦合，实现无需重训练或额外数据收集的多项式时间精确计算。框架在机器人运行前验证所有干预查询的可处理性，自动检测并排除超出支持域的候选。

Result: 在ROS2仿真环境中实验：高质量JPT下因果电路减少10.3%失败尝试；退化JPT下减少37%失败尝试。每次拒绝都能生成结构化的因果报告，指出主要失败变量、观测值及推荐修正区域。

Conclusion: 所提框架支持操作员监督和自主恢复，无需单独训练的失败模型，为安全物理AI的机器人动作测试提供了高效的因果诊断解决方案。

Abstract: Safe physical AI for robot actions are required not only likely to succeed but tested to be safe before execution. In practice, however, formal testing of motion parameters is computationally expensive, and the cost scales poorly with the dimensionality of the action space. When a proposed action is rejected by a tester, the naive response is to resample blindly until a passing candidate is found. This is wasteful, uninformative, and offers no convergence. We argue that rejection should instead trigger causal diagnosis: a principled identification of which action parameter caused the failure and what corrective value maximises the probability of passing testing under the interventional probability distribution. We propose a closed-loop framework that couples a Joint Probability Tree (JPT) with a Causal Circuit derived from a Marginal-Deterministic Variable Tree, enabling exact polytime computation without retraining, or additional data collection. The framework validates tractability of all interventional queries before the robot begins operating, and out-of-support candidates are detected and excluded from correction automatically. We perform experiments in a ROS2 simulation environment, and the framework demonstrates complementary roles across quality of distribution: under a high-quality JPT, the Causal Circuit reduces failed attempts by 10.3% and under a degraded JPT, it reduces total failed attempts by 37%. Every rejected plan produces a structured, interpretable causal report naming the primary cause variable, its observed value, and the recommended corrective region, supporting operator oversight and autonomous recovery without a separately trained failure model.

</details>


### [124] [KineFuse: Kinematic-Aware Haptic Fusion for In-Hand Occluded-Object Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14842)
*Chanyoung Ahn,Jaesung Lee,Sungwoo Park,Donghyun Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于运动学感知的手指级编码器，利用稀疏的触觉信号（本体感知、力/扭矩、接触信息）来辅助预训练视觉追踪器在灵巧手操作中处理物体被手指遮挡时的6D姿态跟踪问题，实验表明该编码器优于多种替代设计，并能学习到特定任务的跨模态门控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手操作需要连续的6D姿态跟踪，但操作手指不可避免地会遮挡相机视野中的物体，导致视觉追踪失效。因此，如何利用多指手上已有的稀疏触觉信号来补充视觉追踪器的不足是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出了一种**运动学感知的手指级编码器（Kinematic-aware Finger-level Encoder）**，将触觉信号（本体感知、力/扭矩、二进制接触）结构化为手指级别的token（4个token），并与预训练的视觉追踪器进行跨模态融合。系统性地对比了四种替代编码器设计（包括平面融合、关节级表示等）。

Result: (i) 逐帧评估无法区分编码器质量，而序列追踪将架构差异放大至15倍；(ii) 结构编码器无需显式监督即可学习到跨模态门控，视觉专攻平移，触觉注意力头专攻旋转；(iii) 4 token的手指级表示优于平面融合和关节级表示。改进的追踪在下游重定向任务中获得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 结构化的手指级触觉编码能够有效补充视觉追踪在遮挡场景下的不足，跨模态门控机制和紧凑的token化设计是提升6D姿态跟踪性能的关键。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires continuous 6D pose tracking, yet the manipulating fingers inevitably occlude the object from the camera. We study how to structure the sparse haptic signals already available on multi-fingered hands, including proprioception, proximal force/torque, and binary contact, to complement a pretrained visual pose tracker under occlusion. We propose a kinematic-aware finger-level encoder and systematically compare it against four alternative designs through three levels of evaluation: per-frame refinement, sequential open-loop tracking, and closed-loop manipulation. Our experiments reveal that (i) per-frame evaluation cannot distinguish encoder quality, while sequential tracking amplifies architectural differences by up to 15 times; (ii) the structured encoder learns task-specific cross-modal gating, using vision exclusively for translation and dedicating one attention head to haptics for rotation, without explicit supervision; and (iii) compact finger-level tokenization with 4 tokens outperforms both flat fusion and joint-level representations, which suppress vision through norm dominance. We validate that improved tracking yields higher success in a downstream reorientation task and provide qualitative real-world demonstrations. Our project page is available at https://cold-young.github.io/kine-fuse/.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Human-like Physical Intelligence: LifelongVision-Language-Action Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14852)
*Yao He,Gan Sun,Wenqi Liang,Fazeng Li,Yang Cong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了LifelongVLA框架，通过双时间尺度LoRA门控模块和缓存高效的重放策略，解决了机器人VLA模型在终身学习中的塑性-稳定性权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人的VLA模型在开放世界部署时需要具备终身学习能力，但现有方法无法有效平衡学习新任务（塑性）和保留旧任务知识（稳定性）之间的权衡。

Method: 1) 提出双时间尺度LoRA门控模块：短时间尺度适配器负责塑性，长时间尺度适配器负责稳定整合，通过任务感知门控显式控制权衡。2) 提出缓存高效随机重放策略，无需完整轨迹存储即可保留均衡的保留信号。

Result: LifelongVLA在多项实验中优于现有基线方法，展示了高效技能扩展、对已学操作行为的鲁棒保留，并减少了在xArm机器人真实部署中对重训练的依赖。

Conclusion: 提出的LifelongVLA框架有效缓解了机器人操作模型中的塑性-稳定性权衡问题，实现了低成本、高效的终身学习部署。

Abstract: Similar to the natural capabilities of humans to sequentially learn new tasks, robots with Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models should possess lifelong learning ability to learn a new task when deployed in open-world environments. However, most recently proposed lifelong learning models aim to effectively learn the current task (plasticity) or maintain high accuracy on previous tasks (stability), while the plasticity-stability trade-off remains largely unsolved in robotic manipulation models. To address this fundamental challenge, we propose a cache-efficient lifelong Vision-Language-Action learning framework for robotic manipulation (i.e., LifelongVLA), which alleviates the plasticity-stability trade-off with a dual-timescale adaptation mechanism while achieving low-cost robotic deployment with a cache-efficient replay strategy. More concretely, we propose a dual-timescale LoRA gating module to decompose VLA adaptation into two lightweight pathways: a short-term adapter for plasticity and a long-term adapter for stable consolidation. These pathways are integrated via a task-aware gate, enabling explicit control of the plasticity-stability trade-off. In the skill replay phase, a cache-efficient stochastic replay strategy is proposed to preserve more balanced retention signals without full-trajectory storage. Finally, experiments show that LifelongVLA outperforms existing baselines, demonstrating efficient skill expansion, robust retention of learned manipulation behaviors, and reduced reliance on retraining for real-world deployment on an xArm robot.

</details>


### [126] [Modeling and Validation of Quality of Control for Edge-Offloaded Collaborative Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14853)
*Neelabhro Roy,Mikael Hammarling,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Gourav Prateek Sharma,Mani H. Dhullipalla,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,James Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对复杂环境下协作控制面临的随机无线延迟和可靠性变化挑战，将质量的控制(QoC)框架扩展到实际机器人模型，通过5G测试实验验证了最优控制-通信协同设计区间，并发现RELIABLE QoS在特定条件下比BEST-EFFORT的QoC性能提升51.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境中的协作控制受到随机无线延迟和可靠性变化的严重影响，这会降低导航、跟踪和避障性能，并导致资源过度配置。现有研究缺乏对端到端网络影响及控制参数与网络延迟-可靠性关系的系统建模和实验验证。

Method: (i) 建模端到端网络对闭环性能的影响；(ii) 系统探索控制参数对网络延迟-可靠性的影响；(iii) 在私有5G测试平台上通过不同延迟、可靠性和控制配置进行实验验证。

Result: 确定了实际机器人的最优控制-通信协同设计区间；在真实条件下比较了标准ROS 2服务质量(QoS)策略的QoC性能，发现RELIABLE QoS在特定实验设置下比BEST-EFFORT的QoC性能提升51.5%。

Conclusion: 通过扩展QoC框架到实际机器人模型并经过5G实验验证，证明了控制-通信协同设计的重要性，并量化了不同QoS策略对协作控制性能的影响差异。

Abstract: Collaborative control in complex environments is severely challenged by stochastic wireless delay and reliability variations, which can degrade navigation, tracking, and collision avoidance. These network-induced uncertainties complicate the maintenance of energy efficiency during collaborative tasks, and can potentially lead to over-provisioning of resources. In this paper, for a navigation setup with dynamic collision avoidance, we address this challenge by expanding the quality of control (QoC) framework from prior works to practical robotic models. Our approach (i) models end-to-end network effects on closed-loop performance, (ii) systematically explores the impact of various control parameters dictating robotic motion on network latency-reliability (iii) validates these models through experiments on a private 5G testbed across varying delay, reliability and control configurations. Our analysis indicates the optimal control-communication co-design operating regimes for practical robots and also compares the QoC performance of standard ROS~2 quality of service (QoS) policies under real-world conditions and showing how RELIABLE QoS offers 51.5% better QoC than BEST-EFFORT under certain experimental settings.

</details>


### [127] [OASIS-Map: Object-Level Change Detection in Multi-Session Mapping using Semantic Correspondence Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14899)
*Haedam Oh,Yifu Tao,Nived Chebrolu,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OASIS-Map 提出了一种基于密集补丁级语义对应的多会话建图系统，能够在机器人重复访问半静态环境时，实现时空一致的物体级别地图更新和变化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期机器人巡检中，环境会因物体的出现、消失、移动或替换而发生变化，静态地图迅速过时。现有变化检测方法在跨重访的物体关联方面仍面临局部视角、遮挡和不完美分割等挑战。

Method: 通过建立时序观测之间的密集补丁级语义对应关系，检测场景变化并逐步关联不同重访次中的物体实例，维护一个时空一致的物体级别地图。

Result: 在3RScan的物体重排场景、停车场中视觉相似的车辆替换场景、以及户外市场的大规模场景变化中进行了验证。在停车场车辆替换场景中变化检测F1达0.783，在3RScan中移动物体关联F1达0.667。

Conclusion: OASIS-Map通过密集语义对应有效解决了跨重访的物体关联问题，在多个真实世界的半静态场景中展现了良好的变化检测和物体关联性能。

Abstract: Map representations which are consistent across repeated visits to a real-world semi-static environment are very useful for long-term robotic inspection. In such settings, the scene may evolve while the robot is absent, with objects appearing, disappearing, moving, or being replaced, quickly making a static map outdated. Existing change-detection methods reason through geometry, category-level semantics, or object persistence. However, achieving reliable object association across revisits remains a key challenge, especially under partial views, occlusion, and imperfect segmentation. In this work, we propose OASIS-Map, a multi-session mapping system that maintains a spatio-temporally consistent object-level map by establishing dense patch-level semantic correspondences between temporal observations. These correspondences detect where the scene has changed and incrementally associate objects across revisits as the robot re-observes the environment. We demonstrate OASIS-Map on three challenging real-world scenarios: object rearrangements in 3RScan, visually similar car replacements in a car park, and large-scale scene changes in an outdoor market. We achieve 0.783 F1 on change detection in a car replacement scenario in a car park and 0.667 F1 on moved object association in 3RScan. https://dynamic.robots.ox.ac.uk/projects/oasis-map/

</details>


### [128] [Human-Robot Interaction in GenAI Architectures via the Agent-Client Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14919)
*Jesus Moncada-Ramirez,Jose-Raul Ruiz-Sarmiento,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出采用Agent-Client Protocol (ACP) 作为人机交互层的统一通信标准，结合MCP形成解耦的三层架构（人机交互层、智能体编排层、物理执行层），实现异构用户界面与机器人系统的互操作性，并支持实时的人类参与工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于GenAI/LLM的机器人架构中，上层人机交互层被专有、临时的接口所碎片化，阻碍了实时人机协作。现有研究缺少统一的HRI通信标准来解决这一碎片化问题。

Method: 提出结合ACP（人机交互层-智能体接口）与MCP（智能体-执行层接口）的三层解耦架构，将人机交互、智能体编排和物理执行分离，实现模块化替换和互操作性。

Result: 在物理移动机器人上进行了实验验证，展示了三种异构用户界面的互操作性，并验证了实时人机协作工作流，延迟开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: ACP可作为统一的HRI通信标准，与MCP结合构成的解耦三层架构能有效解决当前机器人系统中人机交互层碎片化问题，支持灵活、可互操作且支持实时人类参与的机器人系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI), particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), are driving robotic architectures toward agent-based high-level orchestration, in which natural-language instructions can be translated into context-aware action sequences. While the integration of these agents and robotic capabilities is increasingly converging toward standardization through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), the upper Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) layer remains fragmented by proprietary, ad hoc interfaces that hinder real-time human-in-the-loop collaboration.
  To address this fragmentation, this paper proposes the adoption of the Agent-Client Protocol (ACP) -- a communication standard originally introduced for coding agents in software engineering -- as a unified communication contract for the HRI layer in agent-based robotic systems. By combining ACP at the interface-agent link and MCP at the agent-execution link, we formulate a fully decoupled three-layer architecture that separates human interaction, deliberative orchestration, and physical execution. This topology removes rigid architectural dependencies, enabling heterogeneous user interfaces to connect to the same robotic system and allowing the underlying robotic platform to be replaced without requiring client-specific integration changes. Moreover, it provides native support for collaborative HRI capabilities such as real-time observability, explicit human authorization, and immediate task interruption. We experimentally evaluate the proposed architecture on a physical mobile robot, demonstrating interoperability across three heterogeneous user interfaces and validating real-time human-in-the-loop workflows with negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [129] [Steering Robustness into World Action Models via Mechanistic Interpretability and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14943)
*Jihoon Hong,Julian Skifstad,Qiyue Dai,Alice Chan,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文使用机械可解释性研究世界动作模型(WAMs)在分布偏移下的鲁棒性表征，发现部分WAM架构在激活空间中存在低维线性可分的鲁棒性特征，据此提出无训练对比激活引导方法和基于模型最优控制的WA-LQR控制器，在Cosmos-Policy和DiT4DiT模型上验证了有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型(WAMs)虽能实现语义和物理层面的控制，但在分布偏移下表现脆弱，现有方法缺乏对模型内部表征如何影响鲁棒性的深入理解。

Method: 1) 使用机械可解释性分析WAM激活空间中鲁棒性相关扰动的表征；2) 对比成功与失败 rollout 的激活模式；3) 提出对比激活方向实现无训练引导；4) 利用WAM激活动力学的局部线性性质，设计基于模型最优控制的WA-LQR控制器。

Result: 1) 部分WAM架构(Cosmos-Policy、DiT4DiT)的鲁棒性特征在激活空间中呈低维线性可分；2) WA-LQR泛化对比方向到新任务；3) 在相机、夹爪和视觉噪声扰动上优于无引导和提示引导基线。

Conclusion: 基于机械可解释性分析可以预测模型的可引导性，WA-LQR是一种最小侵入性的降阶LQR控制器，能有效提升WAM的鲁棒性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable semantically- and physically-informed control but are brittle under distribution shift. In this work, we use mechanistic interpretability to study how robustness-relevant perturbations are represented in WAM activation space. Comparing activations across successful and unsuccessful rollouts, we find some WAM architectures exhibit low-dimensional linear separability for robustness-critical features, while others do not. This motivates the use of contrastive activation directions for training-free WAM steering. We also show that local linearity in WAM activation dynamics enables efficient feedback steering via model-based optimal control, yielding World-Action Linear Quadratic Regulator (WA-LQR), a minimally-invasive reduced-order LQR controller. Via mechanistic evaluations, we predict strong steerability in the Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT models but weak steerability in LingBot-VA, consistent with steering intervention results. On Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT, WA-LQR generalizes contrastive directions to new tasks and improves robustness to camera, gripper, and visual-noise perturbations over unsteered and prompt steering baselines.

</details>


### [130] [AeroAct: Action-Centered World-Action Models for Language-Conditioned Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14997)
*Xinhong Zhang,Qiyuan Zhu,Yubo Huang,Haolin Chen,Runqing Wang,Yuhao Mo,Zhongxin Chen,Yu Hu,Xinjiang Wang,Jian Sun,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroAct 提出了一种以动作为中心的世界-动作模型（WAM），将预训练的视频扩散Transformer适配到四旋翼飞行器导航中，通过预测局部轨迹-动作块实现语言条件控制，在真实世界飞行中首次验证了WAM的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中视觉语言导航方法大多使用离散动作、高层航点或瞬时速度指令，无法提供关于飞行动作如何改变未来观测的密集监督信号，导致策略难以有效理解语义目标与运动视觉后果之间的因果关系。

Method: AeroAct 将预训练的视频扩散Transformer改造为世界-动作模型，从第一视角视觉历史、本体感知和语言指令中预测局部轨迹-动作块；训练时利用未来第一人称帧作为密集后果监督，部署时直接解码动作；并引入了手持采集设备和自引导流程来提升时序一致性。

Result: 闭环仿真和实物实验表明，时序视觉上下文能提升目标跟踪和目标搜索性能，且基于WAM的策略可以在真实四旋翼飞行器上执行。

Conclusion: AeroAct 首次在真实空中飞行中验证了世界-动作模型的有效性，证明了动作中心建模能够为语言条件四旋翼导航提供更丰富的监督信号和更好的控制性能。

Abstract: Language-conditioned quadrotor flight requires a policy to ground semantic goals, anticipate the visual consequences of ego-motion, and output control references that remain smooth and dynamically executable under rapidly changing first-person views. Existing aerial vision-language navigation and vision-language-action methods commonly use discrete actions, high-level waypoints, or instantaneous velocity commands, which provide limited supervision about how flight actions change future observations. We present AeroAct, an action-centered world-action model (WAM) for quadrotor navigation. To the best of our knowledge, AeroAct is the first WAM instantiated and demonstrated for real-world aerial flight. The model adapts a pretrained video diffusion Transformer to predict local trajectory-action chunks from egocentric visual history, proprioception, and language. Future first-person frames are used during training as dense consequence supervision, while deployment directly decodes actions without generating future video. To obtain aligned visual, state, language, and dynamically feasible action data, we build a DiffAero-based pipeline with complementary Isaac Lab and 3D Gaussian splatting renderers. We further introduce a low-cost handheld collection device that couples camera observations with motion estimates to recreate flight-like egocentric trajectories, and a self-guidance procedure that improves temporal consistency across overlapping trajectory chunks. Closed-loop simulation and real-world experiments show that temporal visual context improves target tracking and object-search performance, and that WAM-based policies can be executed on a physical quadrotor.

</details>


### [131] [CosFly-VLA: A Spatially Aware Vision-Language-Action Model for UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15004)
*Ruilong Ren,Songsheng Cheng,Yunpeng Zhou,Hanxuan Chen,Xiangyue Wang,Tianle Zeng,Shuai Yuan,Binbo Li,Hanzhong Guo,Ji Pei,Da Zhang,Kangli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CosFly-VLA，一种面向无人机遮挡环境下目标跟踪的空间感知视觉-语言-动作模型，通过空间预训练、课程微调、思维链训练和强化学习，在连续遮挡场景下显著提升跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在目标可见时表现良好，但城市环境中建筑物、植被等遮挡物导致目标丢失，模型会朝向错误区域执行动作并在后续观测中放大误差，最终无法重新捕获目标。

Method: 1) 空间接地持续预训练(CPT)：500k混合数据注入深度、距离和3D空间推理能力；2) 三阶段课程监督微调(SFT)：多头预热→两阶段课程学习(常规→长遮挡)；3) 思维链(CoT)训练：学习恢复导向的推理轨迹；4) 闭环强化学习(RL)：多组件奖励优化跟踪行为。

Result: 与OpenVLA相比，CosFly-VLA-0.8B在开环ADE上分别降低34.1%(seen-test)和35.3%(unseen-test)；闭环优化使SR分别提升29.8%和2.5%。

Conclusion: CosFly-VLA实现了从可见帧模仿到空间接地闭环控制的进步，验证了在遮挡场景下结构化预测接口和联合空间推理的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic target tracking is essential for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in complex urban environments, where both the target and the camera viewpoint change continuously. Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies can track visible targets effectively, but their performance often degrades when buildings, vegetation, or roadside objects block the line of sight. During sustained occlusion, a policy may lose the target state, execute actions toward an incorrect region, and amplify this error through subsequent observations until re-acquisition becomes impossible. To this end, we present CosFly-VLA, a spatially aware VLA model that jointly grounds the target, estimates its visibility, and generates continuous flight actions through a structured prediction interface. To train this policy, we use a large-scale recipe over diverse data sources. Spatially Grounded Continued Pretraining (CPT) on a 500k mixed pool injects UAV-view depth, distance, and 3-D spatial reasoning. A three-stage Curriculum-based Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) process then specializes the tracker through multi-head warm-up followed by two-stage curriculum learning over natural and hard / long-occlusion data. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training subsequently teaches recovery-oriented reasoning traces before structured answers. Finally, a closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) stage optimizes tracking behavior with a multi-component reward covering stand-off tracking, grounding quality, collision avoidance, and task success. Relative to OpenVLA, CosFly-VLA-0.8B reduces open-loop Average Displacement Error (ADE) by 34.1% on seen-test and 35.3% on unseen-test. Closed-loop optimization improves Success Rate (SR) by 29.8% and 2.5%, respectively. These results demonstrate progress from visible-frame imitation toward spatially grounded action-closed-loop control, evaluated under a shared oracle state history.

</details>


### [132] [Risk-Aware Belief Control Barrier Functions over Random Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15016)
*Shaohang Han,Gang Chen,Yixi Cai,Ignacio Torroba,Ivan Stenius,Patric Jensfelt,Javier Alonso-Mora,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于风险感知信念控制障碍函数（BCBF）的框架，利用随机有限集（RFS）信念和SMC-PHD滤波器处理未知动态环境中多目标状态不确定性，通过粒子构建非光滑BCBF，保障机器人安全，并在仿真和水下实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知动态环境中，移动物体数量未知且随时间变化，传感器测量存在噪声和不完整性，现有方法难以处理多目标状态不确定性下的安全控制问题。

Method: 1. 利用随机有限集（RFS）信念捕获多目标状态不确定性；2. 采用SMC-PHD滤波器将信念表示为粒子集；3. 基于粒子构建非光滑信念控制障碍函数（BCBF）；4. 建立连续预测下安全集的正向不变性，并推导离散更新保持安全性的显式条件。

Result: 仿真和真实水下实验验证了该方法在保证安全性方面的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 提出的BCBF框架能够有效处理动态环境中多目标状态不确定性带来的安全控制挑战，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Ensuring robot safety in unknown, dynamic environments is a fundamental requirement. It involves inferring the states of an unknown and time-varying number of moving objects from noisy, incomplete measurements. We address safe control under the induced multi-object state uncertainty with a risk-aware belief control barrier function (BCBF) framework. The uncertainty is captured by a random finite set (RFS) belief, estimated by a sequential Monte Carlo probability hypothesis density (SMC-PHD) filter that represents it with a set of particles. Building directly on these particles, we construct a nonsmooth BCBF, establish forward invariance of the safe set under continuous prediction, and derive an explicit condition under which discrete updates preserve safety. Simulation and real-world underwater experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Agile Navigation in Crowded Environments for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15036)
*Shuyu Wu,Zeyu Liu,Tianbao Zhang,Fanxing Li,Fangyu Sun,Mingkang Xiong,Wei Xi,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOP-Nav是一种结合速度障碍( VO )几何安全性与端到端学习敏捷适应性的四足机器人导航系统，通过VOP-Net隐式编码动态约束并预测安全速度区域，在仿真和真实环境中均实现了更高成功率和高效避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人在动态拥挤环境中面临两难：基于模型的方法（如VO）理论上保证安全但依赖准确的障碍物运动估计，在密集人群中易失效；端到端学习方法鲁棒但缺乏障碍物运动预测能力，导致碰撞或保守行为。亟需一种既具备几何安全保障又能灵活适应动态环境的方法。

Method: 提出VOP-Nav系统，包含VOP-Net模块，处理多帧激光雷达数据隐式编码动态约束，基于速度障碍理论预测安全速度区域。VO预测在推理时作为导航策略输入，在训练时作为奖励信号鼓励安全运动。

Result: 在Isaac Gym仿真中，VOP-Nav在成功率和平衡运动速度与避障方面优于所有基线；在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的真实部署验证了其在复杂室内外动态环境中的鲁棒性和高效性。

Conclusion: VOP-Nav成功融合了模型方法的几何安全保证和学习方法的适应能力，避免了显式障碍物检测追踪流程，为四足机器人在密集动态环境中导航提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Navigating dynamic and crowded environments presents significant challenges for quadruped robots due to severe sensor occlusion and unpredictable human motion. Existing approaches face a trade-off: model-based methods, such as Velocity Obstacles (VO), theoretically guarantee safety but rely on accurate obstacle motion estimates that often fail in dense crowds, while end-to-end learning methods offer robustness but lack motion prediction capability of obstacles, leading to collisions or conservative behaviors. To solve this, we propose VOP-Nav, a novel navigation system that combines the geometric safety of VO with the agile adaptability of end-to-end learning. Using only local onboard observations, our system avoids explicit obstacle detection and tracking pipelines. The VOP-Net processes multi-frame LiDAR data to implicitly encode dynamic constraints and predict a safe velocity region derived from Velocity Obstacle theory. Importantly, the VO predictions serve a dual role: they are used as input to the navigation policy during inference and as a reward signal during training to encourage safe motion. Evaluations in Isaac Gym demonstrate that VOP-Nav achieves higher success rates than all baselines while balancing locomotion speed and collision avoidance. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates the system's robustness and efficiency in complex indoor and outdoor dynamic environments.

</details>


### [134] [DriftWorld: Fast World Modeling through Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15065)
*Susie Lu,Haonan Chen,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriftWorld 提出了一种基于漂移生成模型的动作条件世界模型，通过单次前向传播生成未来帧，速度比扩散模型基线快17倍，在多个机器人操作基准上达到最先进决策性能，并可作为离线模拟器实现高达0.99的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散世界模型因多步采样导致推理时生成 rollout 昂贵，限制了大规模动作搜索，迫切需要更高效的生成方式。

Method: 提出 DriftWorld，学习动作条件的漂移（action-conditioned drift），在训练过程中完成漂移学习，推理时仅需单次前向传播即可从当前观察和候选动作序列生成未来帧，避免迭代去噪过程。

Result: 在 Bridge-V2、RT-1、Language Table、Push-T、Robomimic 等基准上达到最先进决策性能；推理速度平均比扩散基线快17倍（30+ fps）；作为离线模拟器时，rollout 评分与真实世界策略相关性高达0.99。

Conclusion: 漂移模型非常适合机器人世界建模，能够在保证生成质量的同时实现快速、高质量的动作想象，直接支持规划和策略评估。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable robots to plan by imagining the outcomes of their actions, but their value for control hinges on generating many rollouts quickly. This creates a bottleneck for diffusion-based world models: multistep sampling makes each rollout expensive, limiting large-scale action search at inference time. We introduce DriftWorld, an action-conditioned world model based on drifting generative models. Rather than denoising iteratively at inference, DriftWorld learns an action-conditioned drift during training, allowing it to generate future frames from the current observation and a candidate action sequence in a single forward pass at 30+ fps, which is 17x faster on average than diffusion based baselines. We evaluate DriftWorld on standard vision-based robotic manipulation benchmarks, including Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, and Robomimic. By producing rollouts that are both accurate and fast, DriftWorld achieves state-of-the-art decision-making performance with far less inference time than diffusion-based world model baselines. Beyond online control, DriftWorld can also serve as an offline simulator for ranking real-world robot policies, with rollout-based scores correlating with ground truth at up to 0.99. These results show that drifting models are a strong fit for robot world modeling, where fast, high-quality imagination directly supports planning and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [135] [Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for Distributed ISAC-Enabled Vehicle Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15111)
*Wenjie Liu,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布式集成感知与通信（ISAC）的车辆协同框架，采用统一的目标导向语义通信（GSC）机制，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波预测车辆状态、掩蔽混合近端策略优化（MHPPO）联合决策感知与通信传输，以及不确定性感知传输设计（UTD），在显著降低信令开销的同时实现100%无碰撞的交叉口车辆协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将感知、通信和控制分开处理，导致冗余传输、状态信息过时以及车辆协同不可靠，亟需一种统一的高效框架。

Method: 提出GSC框架，仅传输语义重要的信号；采用EKF预测和融合分布式感知数据；开发MHPPO基于价值信息（VoI）奖励联合决策感知与通信传输及内容；设计UTD（鲁棒波束赋形+VoI时分功率分配）提升可靠性。

Result: 实现100%无碰撞车辆协同，信令开销显著低于对比基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一GSC框架能有效提升无信号交叉口车辆协同的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Vehicle coordination at unsignalized intersections relies on accurate real-time vehicle state acquisition and reliable command-and-control (C&C) signal delivery. However, existing studies typically treat sensing, communication, and control separately, which may lead to redundant transmissions, outdated state information, and unreliable vehicle coordination. In this paper, we investigate a new scenario of distributed integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled vehicle coordination at intersections, where multiple roadside units (RSUs) collaboratively transmit sensing signals for vehicle state acquisition and C&C signals for vehicle movement control under the management of a central base station (BS). To improve signaling efficiency, we propose a unified goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) framework, which transmits sensing and C&C signals only when they are semantically important for improving intersection traffic throughput. Specifically, an extended Kalman filter (EKF) is adopted to predict vehicle states and fuse distributed sensing measurements. A masked hybrid proximal policy optimization (MHPPO) framework is then developed to jointly determine sensing transmission decisions, C&C transmission decisions, and C&C signal contents based on a value-of-information (VoI) reward. Furthermore, we propose an uncertainty-aware transmission design (UTD), including robust beamforming and VoI-based time-division power allocation, to improve sensing and communication reliability under vehicle state uncertainty and inter-RSU interference. Simulation results show that our proposed framework achieves 100% collision-free vehicle coordination with significantly reduced signaling overhead compared with predictive ISAC baselines adapted from state-of-the-art related studies and several ablation baselines.

</details>


### [136] [Catch, Throw, Repeat: Planning for Human-Robot Partner Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15129)
*Jonathan Rainer Lippert,Kai Ploeger,Abir Chowdhury,Hermann Müller,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机合作抛接球的实时规划与控制架构，使机器人能够与人类伙伴进行多球同步抛接（杂耍）。系统集成了预测性球追踪、自适应在线轨迹优化和状态机协调逻辑。在8名不同水平参与者的用户研究中，系统成功实现了三球级联抛接，所有参与者均超越此前最佳结果，最高记录达到连续20次机器人接球，单球场景下实现100%成功率与连续40次接球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机间动态物体交换（如抛接球）因感知不确定性、时序问题和接触丰富的交互特性而极具挑战。人机合作杂耍是该问题的极端实例，需要精确的实时协调、带反馈控制的预测运动规划以及对人体运动变异性的鲁棒性。发展此类技能对于推动物理人机交互和共享自主性具有重要意义。

Method: 提出实时规划与控制架构，包含三个核心模块：(1) 预测性球追踪；(2) 基于多重打靶法（multiple-shooting）的自适应在线轨迹优化；(3) 基于状态机的协调逻辑，用于实现多球同步的人机合作杂耍。

Result: 在8名不同水平（从初学者到专家）参与者的用户研究中，系统成功实现了人机共享的三球级联杂耍。所有参与者在10分钟内均超越此前报道的最佳结果；其中一名参与者将三球级联杂耍的此前记录提升了5倍（达到连续20次机器人接球），另一名单球场景下实现100%成功率（连续40次接球）。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构能够可靠地实现人机多球同步杂耍，在动态物体交换问题上取得了显著进展，为物理人机交互和共享自主性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Dynamic object exchange between humans and robots remains a challenging problem due to uncertainty in perception, timing, and contact-rich interaction. Human-robot juggling represents a particularly demanding instance of this problem, requiring precise real-time coordination, predictive motion planning with feedback control, and robustness to variability in human motion. Enabling such skills is of interest for advancing physical human-robot interaction and shared autonomy. We present a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling that enables a robot to reliably catch and throw balls in synchronized multi-ball patterns with a human partner. The system integrates predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization using a multiple-shooting formulation, and a state-machine-based coordination logic to enable synchronized multi-ball human-robot partner juggling. In a user study with 8 participants of varying juggling skill from beginner to expert, we demonstrate that our system can achieve three-ball cascades shared between the robot and the human. All participants exceeded previously reported best-case results within a 10-minute test session, with one participant extending the previous record for shared three-ball cascade juggling fivefold to 20 consecutive robot catches, and another participant achieving a 100% success rate with 40 consecutive catches in a single-ball catch-and-return setting. Video documentation can be found at https://kai-ploeger.com/partner-juggling

</details>


### [137] [Assessing Physical Frailty and Fall-Risk Indicators with Social Robots: An in situ Evaluation with Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15156)
*Aniol Civit,Antonio Andriella,Alba Martínez,Joan Ars,Aida Ribera,Cristian Barrué,Guillem Alenyà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于社交机器人的衰弱评估框架，通过行为树架构协调感知、决策、交互与测量模块，结合视觉骨骼追踪执行标准衰弱测试（SPPB、TUG）。经过6个月养老院实地测试（N=81），机器人测量结果与治疗师评估及临床仪器具有高度一致性（ICC>0.9），证明社交机器人可提供可靠、客观的衰弱评估，并采集超越传统指标的额外运动学数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统衰弱评估依赖于人工操作和粗粒度临床指标（如任务完成时间），忽略了生物力学层面的功能衰退标志，且资源消耗大。为解决这一问题，作者提出利用社交机器人实现标准化、多维度、自动化的衰弱评估。

Method: 采用行为树（Behavior Tree）架构协调感知（视觉骨骼追踪）、决策、人机交互和测量模块；机器人引导老年人完成SPPB和TUG测试；与医疗专业人员协同设计；在康复中心研究实验室进行为期6个月、81名老年人的实地评估。

Result: 机器人测量与治疗师评估及临床参考仪器（步态分析步道、IMU）在测试完成时间和步态参数上高度一致（ICC>0.9）；SPPB总分的Cohen's Kappa系数：机器人与治疗师间κ=0.67（显著一致），机器人与IMU间κ=0.55（中度一致）。

Conclusion: 社交机器人能够在医疗环境中提供可靠、客观的衰弱评估，并能采集超越传统临床结局指标的相关运动学指标，为功能衰退监测提供更深入的洞察。

Abstract: Frailty assessments are crucial to evaluate the risk of adverse events and the health and social care needs of older adults, yet their administration remains resource-intensive and typically relies on coarse clinical outcomes, such as task completion times, which may overlook biomechanical indicators of functional decline. To address this, we present a robotic framework that guides older adults through standardised frailty and fall-risk tests while capturing clinical scores and additional frailty-related metrics, offering a deeper insight into a user's condition. The system uses a Behaviour Tree architecture that coordinates perception, decision-making, interaction, and measurement modules. Using vision-based skeleton tracking, the robot evaluates established clinical tests, including the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) and the Timed Up and Go (TUG). The framework was co-designed with healthcare professionals and evaluated in situ during six months in a rehabilitation centre's research lab with N=81 older adults. Robot-derived measurements were compared against therapist assessments and clinical reference instruments, including a gait analysis walkway and an inertial measurement unit (IMU). Results showed excellent agreement for most test completion times and gait-related parameters ($ICC > 0.9$). And, substantial agreement for the overall SPPB score comparing the robot and the therapist ($k = 0.67$) and moderate agreement comparing the robot and the IMU ($k=0.55$). The findings highlight that social robots can provide reliable and objective frailty assessments in healthcare settings while enabling the collection of relevant mobility indicators beyond conventional outcomes.

</details>


### [138] [Scaling Behavior Foundation Model for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15163)
*Weishuai Zeng,Kangning Yin,Xiaojie Niu,Shunlin Lu,Weixiang Zhong,Jiahe Chen,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Zirui Wang,Furui Xu,Ming Zhou,Kailin Li,Weinan Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过协调三种核心组件（运动跟踪学习范式、策略性数据协同、Humanoid Transformer架构）实现行为基础模型（BFM）有效扩展的配方，显著提升了人形机器人控制的精度和泛化能力，在测试集上MPKPE降低10%以上（局部模式）和82%（全局模式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管行为基础模型（BFMs）在人形机器人控制方面展现出潜力，但如何协调学习范式、行为数据和模型架构等关键因素以实现有效的规模扩展仍不明确，本文旨在探索这一扩展配方。

Method: 1) 运动跟踪学习范式：将多样的人形控制问题重新表述为在全局坐标系中复现整体身体行为；2) 策略性协同：在线策略 rollout 数量与参考动作多样性的协同增效；3) Humanoid Transformer：表达能力强、可扩展的模型架构，促进结构化行为表征的自然涌现。

Result: 在仿真和真实部署中，相比现有控制器，局部模式下MPKPE降低超过10%，全局模式下降低82%，控制精度和任务泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: BFM是一种有原则且有效的通用人形机器人控制基础，为实现可扩展的通用人形控制提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid control requires natural whole-body coordination, precise real-time responses to control signals, and robust generalization across diverse environmental contexts, making it a cornerstone for generalist embodied agents. Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have recently emerged as a promising solution to address these challenges by leveraging large-scale behavioral data to achieve superior expressiveness, versatility and generalization. However, despite growing interest in scaling BFMs to further improve their capabilities, it remains unclear how key factors, including the learning paradigm, behavioral data and model architecture should be coordinated to enable effective scaling. In this work, we revisit the scaling recipe for BFMs and demonstrate that substantial performance gains can be achieved through the coordination of three core components: 1) the learning paradigm of motion tracking that reformulates diverse humanoid control problems as the reproduction of integrated whole-body behaviors in the global frame; 2) the strategic synergy between on-policy rollout quantity and reference motion diversity; and 3) the expressive and scalable model architecture termed Humanoid Transformer that facilitates the natural emergence of structured behavioral representations. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world deployment, we demonstrate that our approach yields significant improvements in control fidelity and task generalization, reducing Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE) on the test set by over 10% in local mode and 82% in global mode compared with existing humanoid controllers. These results establish BFM as a principled and effective foundation for scalable and general-purpose humanoid control.

</details>


### [139] [AHEAD: Anticipatory Hand-Driven Teleoperation via Human Intent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15172)
*Seok Joon Kim,Junho Lee,Federica Spinola,Taein Kwon,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AHEAD是一个实时VR遥操作系统，通过注意力分类器预测操作者意图（抓取物体和放置槽位），将手部动作转化为高级指令，使机器人能提前行动，相比基线方法减少了0.6秒（物体）和1.4秒（槽位）的反应延迟，同时降低了操作者负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接手控遥操作精准但慢且累，监督式（目标导向）遥操作虽然简化操作但引入等待延迟。作者想同时降低机器人反应时间和操作者工作负荷。

Method: 提出AHEAD系统：在数字孪生中，操作者通过自然手部动作传达高级指令；利用注意力分类器处理短窗口的3D手部、头部信号和场景上下文，预测意图（抓取物体和放置槽位）；通过状态机将预测转化为稳定的机器人目标，对噪声预测和校正动作具有鲁棒性。

Result: 意图预测Top1准确率：抓取物体76%，目标槽位76%。用户实验中，相比基线，机器人反应延迟减少0.6秒（物体）和1.4秒（槽位），操作者报告更低负荷。

Conclusion: AHEAD通过预测操作者意图实现主动控制，在保持低操作者努力的同时显著降低机器人反应时间，验证了在重复拾放任务中利用意图预测提升遥操作效率的有效性。

Abstract: Direct hand-driven teleoperation maps an operator's hand motion to robot end-effector commands at every frame, enabling precise control, but it requires constant monitoring and correction during approach, grasp, and placement, which can be slow and fatiguing. For repetitive pick-and-place tasks, supervisory (goal-based) teleoperation simplifies this process: the operator specifies goals/waypoints, and the robot executes the motion using planning algorithms. Yet, this introduces latency, as the robot must wait for the next command before it can plan and act. "How can we reduce robot reaction time while lowering operator workload?" To tackle this question, we present AHEAD, a real-time VR teleoperation system that anticipates operator intent to enable proactive, hand-driven control. In a digital twin, the operator performs pick-and-place naturally, using hand motion to convey high-level commands rather than a continuous robot trajectory. AHEAD processes a short window of 3D hand and head signals together with scene context through an attention-based classifier to predict the intended grasp object and placement slot. A state machine converts intent predictions into stable robot goals, enabling early motion while remaining stable under noisy predictions and corrective hand movements. AHEAD's intent prediction module achieves Top1 accuracy: 76% for grasp objects and 76% for target slots. Moreover, our user study shows AHEAD reduces robot reaction latency by 0.6 s (object) and 1.4 s (slot) relative to baselines. Participants also reported lower operator load, indicating faster robot responses while maintaining low operator effort in practice.

</details>


### [140] [RoboTTT: Context Scaling for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15275)
*Yunfan Jiang,Yevgen Chebotar,Ruijie Zheng,Fengyuan Hu,Yunhao Ge,Jimmy Wu,Tianyuan Dai,Scott Reed,Li Fei-Fei,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTTT通过将测试时训练（Test-Time Training）整合到机器人基础模型中，将视觉运动上下文扩展到8000时间步，实现单次模仿人类演示、在线策略改进、鲁棒性提升等新能力，并在实际机器人任务上取得87%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型仅能处理单步或短历史的视觉运动上下文，限制了模型长期记忆和复杂任务执行能力，亟需一种能高效处理超长上下文的方法。

Method: 将测试时训练（TTT）集成到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中，通过梯度下降更新的快速权重作为循环状态压缩历史信息，结合序列动作强制和截断反向传播以扩展训练上下文长度至8K时间步。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务上，整体性能比单步上下文基线提升87%；完整完成5分钟10阶段装配任务（基线从未完成）；8K上下文训练模型比1K上下文模型提升62%。

Conclusion: 上下文长度是机器人基础模型新的可扩展维度，RoboTTT首次证明长上下文预训练能持续提升闭环控制性能，为机器人策略的长期记忆和复杂任务执行开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Recent robot foundation models operate with single-step or short-history visuomotor context. We introduce Test-Time-Training Robot Policies (RoboTTT), a robot model and training recipe that scale visuomotor context to 8K timesteps, three orders of magnitude beyond state-of-the-art policies, without growing inference latency. At this context length, we unlock new robot capabilities: one-shot in-context imitation from human video demonstrations, on-the-fly policy improvement, robustness to perturbations, and stronger performance on multi-stage, long-horizon tasks. We also observe, for the first time, steady gains in closed-loop performance as pretraining context length scales. At its core, RoboTTT integrates Test-Time Training into robot foundation models such as Vision-Language-Action policies, yielding a sequence model whose recurrent state consists of fast weights, parameters updated by gradient descent during both training and inference, compressing histories into weight space and retrieving contextual information for long-context conditioning. To scale training context length, the recipe combines sequence action forcing with truncated backpropagation through time. On challenging real-robot manipulation tasks, RoboTTT improves overall performance by 87% over the single-step context baseline and fully completes a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task, which no baseline ever does. RoboTTT trained with 8K-timestep context outperforms the same model pretrained with 1K timesteps by 62%, suggesting context length as a new scaling axis for robot foundation models. Videos are available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Intelligent Three Level Learning Architecture for Autonomous UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14093)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种三层层次化学习架构用于自主无人机蜂群搜救，整合了三种不同的学习机制（赫布神经可塑性、多智能体强化学习+图神经网络+行为树、模型无关元学习+BDI推理+数字孪生），通过22个架构契约提供6类形式化保证，并引入群体元认知作为组合属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次化强化学习方法在不同层级采用单一学习范式，缺乏生物学启发的多层次学习机制整合，无法满足无人机蜂群搜救任务对安全性、实时性和适应性的综合需求。

Method: 三层层次化架构：底层（反射）使用赫布神经可塑性实现个体适应；中层（技能）使用多智能体强化学习+图神经网络+行为树实现战术协调；顶层（推理）使用模型无关元学习+BDI推理+数字孪生实现战略决策。通过22个架构契约和5个构造性进展函数形式化系统。

Result: 主集成定理证明满足契约时系统可保持6类保证（安全、预算正确性、最优性、活性、无饥饿、层间一致性）；动态情况下5个新契约额外提供3个保证（认知韧性、优雅降级、单调元改进）。解决了现有层次化RL的5个根本性局限。

Conclusion: 该混合神经符号架构通过生物学启发的多层次学习整合，为自主蜂群系统提供了可验证的形式化保证，群体元认知属性使系统具备自我监控和策略切换能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel three level hierarchical learning architecture for autonomous UAV swarms performing search and rescue operations. Unlike conventional approaches that apply a single learning paradigm across all hierarchy levels, the proposed architecture integrates three qualitatively different learning mechanisms corresponding to the biological hierarchy of reflexes, skills, and reasoning such as Hebbian neuroplasticity for individual agent adaptation, multi agent reinforcement learning with graph neural networks and behavior trees for tactical coordination, and model agnostic meta learning with BDI reasoning and a digital twin for strategic decision making.
  The architecture is formalized through twenty two architectural contracts organized across six components such as BDI, Behavior Trees, GNN, MARL, Neuroplasticity, Meta Learning that collectively provide six classes of formal guarantees such as safety, budget correctness, optimality, liveness, starvation freedom, and inter level consistency.
  We introduce Swarm Meta Cognition as a compositional property arising from the structured interaction of all three levels, enabling the swarm to monitor its own cognitive state and switch between cognitive strategies. Five constructive progress functions for SAR task types bridge the gap between abstract optimization theory and concrete operational scenarios.
  The main integration theorem establishes that when all contracts are satisfied, the hybrid neuro-symbolic system preserves all six guarantee classes. For the dynamic case with active learning, five new contracts extend the framework with three additional guarantees such as cognitive resilience, graceful degradation, and monotonic meta improvement. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the architecture addresses five fundamental limitations of existing hierarchical RL approaches.

</details>


### [142] [HG-RAG: Hierarchy-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14095)
*Pranav Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HG-RAG是一个通过遍历层次化知识图谱来增强LLM上下文检索的框架，相比传统平面文档检索，在层次推理、关系推理和多跳推理任务上表现更优，同时减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统从平面文档库中检索上下文，在处理需要层次推理或关系推理的查询时表现不佳，缺乏对结构化知识的利用。

Method: 提出HG-RAG框架，构建层次化知识图谱，检索时先解析查询中的命名实体锚点，然后向上（父节点）、横向（关系邻居）、向下（子节点）遍历图结构，最终将结构化上下文提供给语言模型。

Result: 在三种规模（18-800节点）和四种查询类型（本地事实、层次、邻居、多跳）上，HG-RAG在层次推理、关系推理和多跳推理任务上持续优于平面密集检索基线，同时减少了幻觉并保持局部连贯性。

Conclusion: 层次化知识图谱引导的检索范式能有效弥补传统RAG在结构化推理上的不足，为需要多层级和关系理解的查询提供了更可靠的上下文支持。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has proven to be a widely successful process at improving the quality of outputs from a Large Language Model (LLM) for wider context. However, RAG systems typically retrieve context from flat document stores, which struggles when queries require hierarchical or relational reasoning across structured knowledge. I present HG-RAG (Hierarchy-Guided RAG), a framework that performs graph-traversal over a hierarchical knowledge graph to deliver structured context to a language model. My retrieval pipeline resolves a named entity anchor from the query, then expands context upward through parent nodes, laterally through relational neighbors, and downward through child nodes when needed. I evaluate HG-RAG against a dense retrieval baseline across three world scales (18-800 nodes) with four query types: local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, and multi-hop. Results show HG-RAG consistently outperforms the flat baseline on hierarchical, relational, and multi-hop reasoning tasks, while reducing hallucination and maintaining locality coherence.

</details>


### [143] [IMEX Interaction-Based Model Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14096)
*Emiliano Massi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IMEX方法，通过静态相关力（PCS）和交互相关力（PCI）两个互补指标，在预测建模中识别重要特征及特征间的高阶交互作用，无需限制交互阶数，能构建模型预测的可解释性图谱。实验在三个合成数据集上与INVASE对比，验证了PCS在非线性、条件依赖和多重共线性关系下恢复特征结构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒模型虽然预测准确，但缺乏透明性，难以解释其内部决策机制。在关键应用场景中，仅靠预测精度不足以作为有效的验证指标，需要能够揭示模型决策原因的解释方法。

Method: IMEX框架基于两个互补指标：静态相关力（PCS）量化单个特征的贡献，交互相关力（PCI）捕捉特征间的非加性效应。该方法不限制交互分析的阶数，可研究任意高阶特征子集的交互作用，最终构建预测的完整可解释性图谱。

Result: 在三个具有已知结构的合成数据集上，将IMEX的PCS组件与INVASE方法进行对比实验。结果表明，当输入特征与预测目标之间存在非线性、条件依赖和多重共线性关系时，IMEX能够有效恢复相关的特征层级结构。

Conclusion: IMEX为可解释预测建模提供了一个有效的方法论方向，不仅能够识别重要特征，还能揭示与潜在影响机制一致的交互模式，在复杂关系场景下优于或可比于现有方法。

Abstract: In predictive modeling, the ability to explain why a model produces a given target prediction has become increasingly important [5, 10]. Black-box models do not provide a transparent description of the internal mechanisms that generate the prediction, making even accurate predictions difficult to interpret and validate. In critical contexts, predictive accuracy alone is not a sufficient validation metric if the reasons underlying model decisions remain unexplained. The IMEX (Interaction-Based Model Explanation) approach represents a methodological direction within explainable predictive modeling. IMEX is designed to identify which variables contribute most to the target prediction and which interactions among variables are significant in determining the target. The method does not impose limitations on higher-order interaction analysis, allowing the investigation of feature subsets with cardinality greater than two. Beyond the identification of feature importance, IMEX enables the exploration of interaction patterns that may be consistent with latent mechanisms influencing the outcome. Through the application of the IMEX algorithm, it is possible to construct an interpretability map of the predictions. The IMEX framework is built on two complementary metrics: Static Correlation Power (PCS), which quantifies the contribution of individual features, and Interaction Correlation Power (PCI), which captures non-additive effects among features. In the present work, the PCS component is experimentally validated through a comparison with INVASE [18] on three synthetic datasets with known structures. The results indicate that IMEX can recover relevant feature-level structures in the presence of non-linear, conditional, and multicollinear relationships between input features and prediction targets.

</details>


### [144] [RegNetAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Network Regulatory Driver Identification in Cancer Genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14097)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RegNetAgents 是一个基于多智能体框架的调控候选基因识别系统，整合TCGA肿瘤网络和GREmLN单细胞网络，通过双重网络分类、癌症基因过滤和作用模式分配，对调控候选基因进行排序，在乳腺癌和结直肠癌中显著富集OncoKB注释的癌症基因，支持从候选基因识别到生物学假设生成的端到端解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调控网络分析通常局限于单一数据源（如bulk肿瘤或单细胞），缺乏跨异构网络的统一分析框架来系统性识别调控候选基因，难以全面捕捉复杂的基因调控关系。

Method: 构建多智能体LangGraph DAG工作流，集成TCGA肿瘤网络和GREmLN单细胞网络，对目标基因进行双重网络分类、基于OncoKB的癌症基因过滤以及作用模式(MoA)分配，根据网络间证据一致性（Both/TCGA-only/GREmLN-only）对候选基因排序。

Result: 在11个乳腺癌和12个结直肠癌目标基因中，TCGA来源候选基因显著富集（BRCA: Z=6.69; COAD: Z=6.95），GREmLN来源也显著富集（BRCA: Z=5.51; COAD: Z=7.06, p<0.0001），且在管家基因和非驱动基因对照中无富集，验证了信号特异性。

Conclusion: RegNetAgents 建立了一个可解释的AI框架，能够跨异构调控网络进行癌症基因组学中的调控候选基因识别，并支持致癌潜力、药物可及性、临床相关性和网络脆弱性的结构化评估，实现端到端的生物学假设生成。

Abstract: We introduce RegNetAgents, an AI-oriented multi-agent framework for structured, query-driven regulatory candidate identification across heterogeneous gene regulatory networks. The system enables unified analysis of bulk tumor and single-cell-derived ARACNe networks by integrating TCGA-derived cancer networks with large-scale single-cell regulatory networks from the GREmLN project.
